📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期禽流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 研究发现,预先存在的免疫力会降低病毒载量并阻断 传播,从而导致对病毒多样性的强烈选择。不同的组织施加不同的选择压力,上呼吸道有利于与人类受体高亲和力结合的病毒,而下呼吸道有利于抗体逃逸病毒。 [10.1002/advs.78843]。
  • 两种 疫苗 均诱导 HI 反应,并在第 3-4 周达到峰值。rgH5N1 疫苗 引发针对 H5N8 的高交叉反应滴度 (9.2–9.4 log 2 ),而 rgH5N8 疫苗 引发针对 H5N1 的较低交叉反应滴度 (7.25 log 2 )。
  • 该病毒引起 SPF 鸡的轻微临床疾病和 100% 存活率,但观察到显着的呼吸道组织病理学损伤。在 BALB/c 小鼠中,它表现出有限的鼻腔复制,表明哺乳动物的适应潜力较低。 [10.1186/s13567-026-01862-3]。
  • 疫苗 诱导显着的血清体液反应,在 H5N1 攻击后,它为大多数鸡提供保护并防止病毒脱落。 [10.3390/v18111285]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The study found that pre-existing immunity reduces viral load and blocks transmission, leading to strong selection on viral diversity. [10.1002/advs.78843].
  • Both vaccines induced HI responses that peaked at weeks 3–4.
  • The virus caused mild clinical disease and 100% survival in SPF chickens, but significant respiratory tract histopathological damage was observed.
  • The vaccine induced a significant serum humoral response, and after the H5N1 challenge, it provided protection and prevented virus shedding in most of the chickens. [10.3390/v18111285].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 印度计划于2026年11月20日推出首个针对家禽的本土H5N1禽流感疫苗,以应对持续的禽流感暴发。
  • 疫苗由ICAR-NIHSAD研发,针对H5N1进化枝2.3.2.1a,该毒株导致多数禽流感暴发。
  • 疫苗预计可减少家禽死亡和病毒传播,但部分接种家禽仍可能传播病毒。
  • 疫苗的推出标志着印度在禽流感防控方面的重要进展,但具体实施细节尚未公布。
  • 自2006年以来,印度已报告超过500起H5N1和H5N8禽流感暴发,造成重大经济损失。
  • India is set to unveil its first indigenous H5N1 bird flu vaccine for poultry on November 20, 2026, as reported by multiple sources.
  • The vaccine, developed by ICAR-NIHSAD, targets the H5N1 Clade 2.3.2.1a strain, responsible for most outbreaks in the country.
  • While the vaccine aims to reduce poultry deaths and virus shedding, some vaccinated birds may still shed the virus.
  • The vaccine is a significant step in strengthening India's disease control and biosecurity measures.
  • Over 500 outbreaks involving H5N1 and H5N8 have been reported since 2006, causing substantial economic losses.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Emerging Infectious Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-12-01  |  DOI: 10.3201/eid3212.260869  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 32, 12, 2116-2118

2024–2026 年埃及牲畜中 禽流感 病毒暴露的血清学证据

Serologic Evidence of Avian Influenza Virus Exposure in Livestock, Egypt, 2024–2026
作者： Mokhtar Gomaa, R. Elshesheny, Ahmed El Taweel, Omnia Kutkat, Hager Emam, Basant Helal, Ayat Swelam, Mohamed GabAllah, Ahmed Kandeil, Pamela P. McKenzie
摘要中文翻译美国牛群感染甲型H5N1流感病毒跨物种溢出引发全球关注。我们调查了地方性 禽流感 病毒是否可能蔓延到埃及的家畜中。我们在 2.5% 的测试动物中检测到 H9N2 流感抗体,在 2.9%-4.5% 的测试动物中检测到 H5N1 抗体。其他 监测 将阐明 宿主 感染动态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查地方性 禽流感 病毒是否可能蔓延到埃及的家畜中,原因是全球对 A (H5N1) 流感病毒 跨物种溢出 感染美国牛的担忧。
设计与对象
该研究于 2024 年至 2026 年间在埃及进行,重点关注家畜,以评估 禽流感 病毒的暴露情况。
核心方法
研究人员使用血清学检测方法测试了牲畜样本中针对特定 禽流感 亚型的抗体。
主要结果
2.5% 的测试动物中检测到 H9N2 流感抗体,而 2.9%-4.5% 的测试群体中检测到 H5N1 抗体。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,禽流感 病毒可能在家畜中传播,突出表明需要进一步调查 宿主 感染的动态。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明额外的 监测 对于更好地了解 传播 和 禽流感 病毒在牲畜群体中的感染动态具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究对其研究结果具有中等可信度,因为它基于血清学证据,不包括详细的纵向或分子数据。

Serologic Evidence of Avian Influenza Virus Exposure in Livestock, Egypt, 2024–2026

Authors: Mokhtar Gomaa, R. Elshesheny, Ahmed El Taweel, Omnia Kutkat, Hager Emam, Basant Helal, Ayat Swelam, Mohamed GabAllah, Ahmed Kandeil, Pamela P. McKenzie
Original abstract (English)
Spillover of influenza A(H5N1) virus to cattle in the United States sparked global concern. We investigated whether endemic avian influenza viruses are potentially spilling over into domestic livestock in Egypt. We detected influenza H9N2 antibodies in 2.5% and H5N1 antibodies in 2.9%-4.5% of tested animals. Additional surveillance will elucidate host infection dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether endemic avian influenza viruses are potentially spilling over into domestic livestock in Egypt, driven by global concern over the spillover of influenza A(H5N1) virus to cattle in the United States.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Egypt between 2024 and 2026, focusing on domestic livestock to assess exposure to avian influenza viruses.
Core methods
The researchers tested livestock specimens for antibodies against specific avian influenza subtypes using serologic assays.
Main results
Influenza H9N2 antibodies were detected in 2.5% of tested animals, while H5N1 antibodies were found in 2.9%-4.5% of the tested population.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that avian influenza viruses may be circulating in livestock, highlighting the need for further investigation into the dynamics of host infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform the importance of additional surveillance to better understand the transmission and infection dynamics of avian influenza viruses in livestock populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study provides moderate confidence in its findings, as it is based on serologic evidence and does not include detailed longitudinal or molecular data.

鸡的解剖区室化驱动不同的 H9N2 流感 进化 走向人类受体结合和免疫逃逸。

Anatomical Compartmentalization in Chickens Drives Divergent H9N2 Influenza Evolution Toward Human Receptor Binding and Immune Escape.
作者： Wei Chen, Lingkai Zhang, Xinyi Pei, Chaoxuan Guo, Qingshuai Sun, Xinyu Gao, Rizwan Ullah, Xu Zhou, Xianying Zeng, Guobin Tian
摘要中文翻译摘要 禽流感 A(H9N2) 病毒具有大流行威胁,它优先结合人类受体并具有逃避鸡的免疫力的能力。然而,驱动这些特征的选择性力量仍不清楚。在这里,我们展示了鸡体内的解剖结构如何塑造血凝素 (HA) 进化。使用深度突变扫描结合体内研究,我们发现预先存在的免疫力降低了病毒载量并阻断了 传播,从而对病毒多样性施加了强烈的选择。不同的组织对免疫鸡产生不同的选择压力。上呼吸道主要富集与人类受体高亲和力结合的病毒,而下呼吸道则优先富集抗体逃逸病毒。这种功能差异通过携带定义替换的变体进行了验证,这表明其适应性与各自的区室一致。我们的结果表明,禽类 宿主 内的组织特异性选择可以同时塑造病毒多样性以适应人类和免疫逃避,为在家禽中出现具有大流行潜力的变异提供了机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解驱动 禽流感 A(H9N2) 病毒的 进化 与人类受体结合和鸡免疫逃逸的选择性力量,解决这些性状背后机制的知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究以鸡为对象,利用深度突变扫描和体内研究来研究预先存在的免疫力和组织特异性选择压力对病毒 进化 的影响。
核心方法
研究人员采用深度突变扫描和体内研究来评估预先存在的免疫力和组织特异性选择对病毒多样性和适应性的影响。
主要结果
研究发现,预先存在的免疫力会降低病毒载量并阻断 传播,从而导致对病毒多样性的强烈选择。不同的组织施加不同的选择压力,上呼吸道有利于与人类受体高亲和力结合的病毒,而下呼吸道有利于抗体逃逸病毒。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,禽类 宿主 内的组织特异性选择可以同时塑造病毒多样性以适应人类和免疫逃避,为家禽中出现大流行潜在变异提供了机制。
科研与公卫意义
结果对 监测、诊断 和 禽流感 的预防具有影响,特别是在潜在的人类感染和大流行风险的背景下。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性包括使用单一 禽流感 A(H9N2) 病毒株,以及需要在更广泛的病毒株和 宿主 中进行进一步验证。由于依赖单一病毒株并且需要额外的研究来确认研究结果的普遍性,因此证据强度中等。

Anatomical Compartmentalization in Chickens Drives Divergent H9N2 Influenza Evolution Toward Human Receptor Binding and Immune Escape.

Authors: Wei Chen, Lingkai Zhang, Xinyi Pei, Chaoxuan Guo, Qingshuai Sun, Xinyu Gao, Rizwan Ullah, Xu Zhou, Xianying Zeng, Guobin Tian
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Avian influenza A(H9N2) viruses pose a pandemic threat, having acquired preferential binding to human receptors and the ability to evade immunity in chickens. However, the selective forces driving these traits remain unclear. Here, we show how anatomical compartmentalization within chickens shapes hemagglutinin (HA) evolution. Using deep mutational scanning coupled with in vivo studies, we find that pre‐existing immunity reduces viral load and blocks transmission, thereby imposing strong selection on viral diversity. Distinct tissues exert divergent selection pressures in immunized chickens. The upper respiratory tract mainly enriches viruses with high‐affinity binding to human‐type receptors, whereas the lower respiratory tract preferentially favors antibody‐escape viruses. This functional difference was validated using variants carrying defined substitutions, which showed fitness aligned with their respective compartment. Our results demonstrate that tissue‐specific selection within an avian host can concurrently shape viral diversity toward human adaptation and immune evasion, providing a mechanism for the emergence of variants with pandemic potential in poultry.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the selective forces driving the evolution of avian influenza A(H9N2) viruses toward human receptor binding and immune escape in chickens, addressing a knowledge gap in the mechanisms underlying these traits.
Design and population
The study was conducted in chickens, using deep mutational scanning and in vivo studies to investigate the effects of pre-existing immunity and tissue-specific selection pressures on viral evolution.
Core methods
The researchers employed deep mutational scanning and in vivo studies to assess the impact of pre-existing immunity and tissue-specific selection on viral diversity and fitness.
Main results
The study found that pre-existing immunity reduces viral load and blocks transmission, leading to strong selection on viral diversity. Different tissues exert distinct selection pressures, with the upper respiratory tract favoring viruses with high-affinity binding to human-type receptors and the lower respiratory tract favoring antibody-escape viruses.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that tissue-specific selection within an avian host can concurrently shape viral diversity toward human adaptation and immune evasion, providing a mechanism for the emergence of pandemic potential variants in poultry.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results have implications for surveillance, diagnosis, and prevention of avian influenza, particularly in the context of potential human infection and pandemic risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations include the use of a single avian influenza A(H9N2) virus strain and the need for further validation in a broader range of viral strains and hosts. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on a single virus strain and the need for additional studies to confirm the generalizability of the findings.

孟加拉国的 H9N2 禽流感 病毒:三个遗传上不同的共循环亚群保留了很大程度上保守的抗原表型

H9N2 avian influenza virus in Bangladesh: three genetically distinct co-circulating subgroups retain a largely conserved antigenic phenotype
作者： Rebecca Daines, Jean-Remy Sadeyen, Mahmudul Hasan, Tridip Das, Rashed Mahmud Sizer, Md. Helal Uddin, Paritosh Kumar Biswas, Mohammed Abdus Samad, Md. Ahasanul Hoque, Anne Conan
摘要中文翻译摘要 低致病性 禽流感 (LPAI) H9N2 病毒在孟加拉国家禽中流行,造成重大经济损失并构成持续的人畜共患风险。尽管遗传监测已经记录了正在进行的病毒进化,但遗传多样性和抗原变化之间的关系仍然知之甚少。这项研究调查了孟加拉国流行的当代 H9N2 病毒的遗传多样性、重排和抗原特征。对 219 个采样序列的系统发育分析揭示了三个遗传上不同的单系血凝素 (HA) G 谱系亚群,每个亚群都以独特的氨基酸取代为特征。相比之下,神经氨酸酶(NA)表现出相似的遗传多样性,但没有明显的系统发育簇。对配对 HA 和 NA 系统发育的双度量分析确定了 20 种假定的重配 H9N2 病毒。进一步分析表明,遗传多样性具有地理结构;然而,所有三个HA亚群在多个地区共同流通,并且一些活禽市场同时包含所有三个亚群的代表,这表明活禽市场可能促进遗传上不同的病毒之间的混合,并为其他地理上分离的毒株之间的重配创造机会。尽管存在大量的遗传多样性和重配,但根据血凝抑制和病毒中和测定,所有病毒在抗原上仍然相似。然而,一个亚组的代表性菌株诱导的同源和异源抗体滴度显着降低,表明免疫原性降低。我们的研究结果表明,孟加拉国 H9N2 病毒的 进化 基因与抗原变化之间存在显着脱节。虽然重配和遗传多样化继续塑造病毒种群,但抗原特性在很大程度上仍然保守,支持当前 疫苗 策略的持续相关性,同时强调持续遗传和抗原 监测 的重要性。重要性 低致病性 禽流感 (LPAI) H9N2 病毒在孟加拉国仍然流行,并通过遗传多样化和重组继续进化。这项研究表明,尽管出现了三个不同的 HA 遗传亚群和频繁的重配事件,当代孟加拉国 H9N2 病毒仍然保持抗原保守性。这些发现揭示了遗传 进化 和抗原变化之间的重要脱节,表明仅遗传差异可能无法预测 疫苗 的有效性。活禽市场内多个病毒亚群的共同循环凸显了它们作为病毒混合和重组中心的作用,强调了在这些环境中需要有针对性的 监测。虽然目前的 疫苗 策略可能对流行菌株仍然有效,但免疫原性降低的亚组的鉴定强调了持续监测遗传和表型特征的重要性。总之,这些发现为支持孟加拉国及其他地区的风险评估、疫苗 评估和 禽流感 控制计划提供了关键证据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查孟加拉国 H9N2 病毒的遗传多样性、重排和抗原特征,因为这些病毒在家禽中流行,并具有人畜共患风险。
设计与对象
该研究分析了从孟加拉国家禽中收集的 219 个 H9N2 病毒样本序列,重点关注它们在不同地理区域的遗传和抗原特征。
核心方法
系统发育分析用于评估遗传多样性和重配,而血凝抑制和病毒中和测定则评估抗原特征。
主要结果
系统发育分析揭示了三个遗传上不同的HA亚群,但所有病毒在抗原上仍然相似。一个亚组显示出免疫原性降低,因为它诱导的抗体滴度显着降低。
解释与创新
作者解释说,遗传 进化 并不一定会导致抗原变化,这凸显了遗传多样性与 疫苗 有效性之间的新脱节。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持孟加拉国当前针对 H9N2 病毒的 疫苗 策略的持续相关性,并强调持续遗传和抗原 监测 的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据基于家禽的序列和抗原数据,可能无法完全代表人畜共患 传播 动态,并且由于分析的观察性质,证据强度中等。

H9N2 avian influenza virus in Bangladesh: three genetically distinct co-circulating subgroups retain a largely conserved antigenic phenotype

Authors: Rebecca Daines, Jean-Remy Sadeyen, Mahmudul Hasan, Tridip Das, Rashed Mahmud Sizer, Md. Helal Uddin, Paritosh Kumar Biswas, Mohammed Abdus Samad, Md. Ahasanul Hoque, Anne Conan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Low pathogenic avian influenza (LPAI) H9N2 viruses are endemic in poultry across Bangladesh, causing substantial economic losses and posing a persistent zoonotic risk. Although genetic surveillance has documented ongoing viral evolution, the relationship between genetic diversification and antigenic change remains poorly understood. This study investigated the genetic diversity, reassortment, and antigenic characteristics of contemporary H9N2 viruses circulating in Bangladesh. Phylogenetic analysis of 219 sampled sequences revealed three genetically distinct monophyletic haemagglutinin (HA) G-lineage sub-groups, each characterised by unique amino acid substitutions. In contrast, neuraminidase (NA) displayed similar genetic diversity without distinct phylogenetic clusters. A dual-metric analysis of paired HA and NA phylogenies identified 20 putative reassortant H9N2 viruses. Further analysis showed that genetic diversity was geographically structured; however, all three HA subgroups co-circulated across multiple regions, and some live bird markets contained representatives of all three groups simultaneously, suggesting that live bird markets may facilitate mixing between genetically distinct viruses, and create opportunities for reassortment among otherwise geographically separated strains. Despite substantial genetic diversity and reassortment, all viruses remained antigenically similar based on haemagglutination inhibition and virus neutralisation assays. However, a representative strain from one subgroup induced significantly lower homologous and heterologous antibody titres, suggesting reduced immunogenicity. Our findings demonstrate a notable disconnect between genetic evolution and antigenic change in Bangladeshi H9N2 viruses. While reassortment and genetic diversification continue to shape viral populations, antigenic properties remain largely conserved, supporting the continued relevance of current vaccine strategies while highlighting the importance of ongoing genetic and antigenic surveillance. Importance Low pathogenic avian influenza (LPAI) H9N2 viruses remain endemic in Bangladesh and continue to evolve through genetic diversification and reassortment. This study demonstrates that, despite the emergence of three distinct HA genetic subgroups and frequent reassortment events, contemporary Bangladeshi H9N2 viruses remain antigenically conserved. These findings reveal an important disconnect between genetic evolution and antigenic change, indicating that genetic divergence alone may not predict vaccine effectiveness. The co-circulation of multiple viral subgroups within live bird markets highlights their role as hubs for viral mixing and reassortment, emphasizing the need for targeted surveillance in these settings. While current vaccine strategies are likely to remain effective against circulating strains, the identification of a subgroup with reduced immunogenicity underscores the importance of continued monitoring of both genetic and phenotypic traits. Together, these findings provide critical evidence to support risk assessment, vaccine evaluation, and avian influenza control programmes in Bangladesh and beyond.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the genetic diversity, reassortment, and antigenic characteristics of H9N2 viruses in Bangladesh, as these viruses are endemic in poultry and pose a zoonotic risk.
Design and population
The study analyzed 219 sampled sequences of H9N2 viruses collected from poultry in Bangladesh, focusing on their genetic and antigenic features across different geographic regions.
Core methods
Phylogenetic analysis was used to assess genetic diversity and reassortment, while haemagglutination inhibition and virus neutralisation assays evaluated antigenic characteristics.
Main results
Phylogenetic analysis revealed three genetically distinct HA subgroups, but all viruses remained antigenically similar. One subgroup showed reduced immunogenicity, as it induced significantly lower antibody titres.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that genetic evolution does not necessarily lead to antigenic change, highlighting a novel disconnect between genetic diversity and vaccine effectiveness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the continued relevance of current vaccine strategies against H9N2 viruses in Bangladesh and emphasize the need for ongoing genetic and antigenic surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on sequence and antigenic data from poultry, which may not fully represent zoonotic transmission dynamics, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the analysis.

灭活重组 H5N1 和 H5N8 疫苗 对鸡高致病性 禽流感 H5 型菌株的保护和交叉保护功效

Protective and Cross-Protective Efficacies of Inactivated Recombinant H5N1 and H5N8 Vaccines Against Highly-Pathogenic Avian Influenza H5-Type Strains in Chickens
作者： Mohamed N. F. Shaheen, Adham Alkashef, Vinayak Brahmakshatriya, Hassan Abushanab, Walid Hamdy Hassan
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 和 H5N8 病毒(进化枝 2.3.4.4b)的共循环威胁家禽生产和公共卫生,因此需要针对这两种病毒的 疫苗。本研究评估了灭活重组(反向遗传学衍生)rgH5N1 和 rgH5N8 疫苗(10 8.0 50% 胚胎感染剂量 [EID 50 ]/剂量)对无特定病原体 (SPF) 鸡的同源和异源 HPAI H5 型攻击的免疫原性和交叉保护功效。疫苗 菌株用 0.2% 福尔马林灭活并用 Montanide ISA 71 VG 佐剂乳化。SPF 鸡(每组 n = 10 只)接受 rgH5N1 (G1)、rgH5N8 (G2) 或磷酸盐缓冲盐水(G3,未接种疫苗的对照)。在 疫苗接种 后第 2、3 和 4 周测量血凝抑制 (HI) 滴度。在第4周,用同源或异源HPAI病毒(10 6 EID 50 /0.1 mL)鼻内攻击鸡,并监测10天的临床症状和死亡率;通过实时 RT-PCR 评估病毒脱落情况。两种 疫苗 均诱导 HI 反应,并在第 3-4 周达到峰值。rgH5N1 疫苗 引发针对 H5N8 的高交叉反应滴度 (9.2–9.4 log 2 ),而 rgH5N8 疫苗 引发针对 H5N1 的较低交叉反应滴度 (7.25 log 2 )。在同源和异源攻击后,疫苗 均具有 100% 的存活率,并且未检测到病毒脱落(循环阈值 [Ct] > 38)。所有未接种疫苗的对照均死亡,从第 3 天起检测到高病毒载量。两种灭活的 疫苗 在受控条件下提供针对异源 H5 型攻击的完全交叉保护,防止 死亡率 和可检测到的病毒脱落。这支持了它们在家禽中控制共循环 H5N1 和 H5N8 病毒的潜力,尽管需要在现场条件下进行更大群体的验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 和 H5N8 病毒的共同传播威胁家禽生产和公共卫生,因此需要针对这两种病毒的 疫苗。
设计与对象
本研究评估了灭活重组(反向遗传学衍生)rgH5N1 和 rgH5N8 疫苗 对无特定病原体 (SPF) 鸡的同源和异源 HPAI H5 型攻击的免疫原性和交叉保护功效。
核心方法
疫苗 菌株用 0.2% 福尔马林灭活并用 Montanide ISA 71 VG 佐剂乳化。在 疫苗接种 后第 2、3 和 4 周测量血凝抑制 (HI) 滴度。
主要结果
两种 疫苗 均诱导 HI 反应,并在第 3-4 周达到峰值。rgH5N1 疫苗 引发针对 H5N8 的高交叉反应滴度 (9.2–9.4 log 2 ),而 rgH5N8 疫苗 引发针对 H5N1 的较低交叉反应滴度 (7.25 log 2 )。
解释与创新
两种灭活的 疫苗 在受控条件下提供针对异源 H5 型攻击的完全交叉保护,防止 死亡率 和可检测的病毒脱落。
科研与公卫意义
这支持了它们在家禽中控制共循环 H5N1 和 H5N8 病毒的潜力,尽管需要在现场条件下进行更大群体的验证。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未明确报告局限性或证据强度,但该研究是在受控条件下进行的,并且结果基于小样本量(每组 n = 10)。

Protective and Cross-Protective Efficacies of Inactivated Recombinant H5N1 and H5N8 Vaccines Against Highly-Pathogenic Avian Influenza H5-Type Strains in Chickens

Authors: Mohamed N. F. Shaheen, Adham Alkashef, Vinayak Brahmakshatriya, Hassan Abushanab, Walid Hamdy Hassan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Co-circulation of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 and H5N8 viruses (clade 2.3.4.4b) threatens poultry production and public health, so vaccines that protect against both viruses are needed. This study evaluated the immunogenicity and cross-protective efficacy of inactivated recombinant (reverse genetics-derived) rgH5N1 and rgH5N8 vaccines (10 8.0 50% embryo infectious dose [EID 50 ]/dose) against homologous and heterologous HPAI H5-type challenge in specific-pathogen-free (SPF) chickens. Vaccine strains were inactivated with 0.2% formalin and emulsified with Montanide ISA 71 VG adjuvant. SPF chickens (n = 10 per group) received rgH5N1 (G1), rgH5N8 (G2), or phosphate-buffered saline (G3, unvaccinated controls). Hemagglutination inhibition (HI) titers were measured at weeks 2, 3, and 4 post-vaccination. At week 4, birds were challenged intranasally with homologous or heterologous HPAI virus (10 6 EID 50 /0.1 mL) and monitored for 10 days for clinical signs and mortality; viral shedding was assessed by real-time RT-PCR. Both vaccines induced HI responses that peaked at weeks 3–4. The rgH5N1 vaccine elicited high cross-reactive titers against H5N8 (9.2–9.4 log 2 ), whereas the rgH5N8 vaccine elicited lower cross-reactive titers against H5N1 (7.25 log 2 ). Both vaccines conferred 100% survival and no detectable viral shedding (cycle threshold [Ct] > 38) after homologous and heterologous challenge. All unvaccinated controls died, with high viral loads detected from day 3. Both inactivated vaccines provided complete cross-protection against heterologous H5-type challenge under controlled conditions, preventing mortality and detectable viral shedding. This supports their potential for controlling co-circulating H5N1 and H5N8 viruses in poultry, although confirmation in larger groups under field conditions is needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Co-circulation of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 and H5N8 viruses threatens poultry production and public health, so vaccines that protect against both viruses are needed.
Design and population
This study evaluated the immunogenicity and cross-protective efficacy of inactivated recombinant (reverse genetics-derived) rgH5N1 and rgH5N8 vaccines against homologous and heterologous HPAI H5-type challenge in specific-pathogen-free (SPF) chickens.
Core methods
Vaccine strains were inactivated with 0.2% formalin and emulsified with Montanide ISA 71 VG adjuvant. Hemagglutination inhibition (HI) titers were measured at weeks 2, 3, and 4 post-vaccination.
Main results
Both vaccines induced HI responses that peaked at weeks 3–4. The rgH5N1 vaccine elicited high cross-reactive titers against H5N8 (9.2–9.4 log 2 ), whereas the rgH5N8 vaccine elicited lower cross-reactive titers against H5N1 (7.25 log 2 ).
Interpretation and novelty
Both inactivated vaccines provided complete cross-protection against heterologous H5-type challenge under controlled conditions, preventing mortality and detectable viral shedding.
Scientific and public-health significance
This supports their potential for controlling co-circulating H5N1 and H5N8 viruses in poultry, although confirmation in larger groups under field conditions is needed.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength explicitly, but the study was conducted under controlled conditions and the results are based on a small sample size (n = 10 per group).

一种新型 H6N2 禽流感 重配病毒的表征和 疫苗 诱导的免疫,该病毒具有 H9N2 衍生的 NA 和来自中国河南家禽的内部基因

Characterization and vaccine-induced immunity of a novel H6N2 avian influenza reassortant virus with H9N2-derived NA and internal genes from poultry in Henan, China
作者： Lin Liu, Yu-Man Liu, Xiao-Jie Zhou, Hang Liao, Xue Pei, Yu-Feng Ren, Wen-Wen Zhou, Wen-Ming Gao, Jing-Rui Liu, Jing-Jing Chang
摘要中文翻译摘要 2025 年 12 月,从中国河南的一只鸡 暴发 中分离到 H6N2 死亡率 病毒(A/chicken/Henan/NL04/2025,NL04),该病毒具有 15% 的田 死亡率 并伴有严重呼吸道疾病。基因组 测序证实它是一种新型重配体,具有水禽来源的 H6 衍生的 HA、H9N2 衍生的 NA 和 H9N2 G57 基因型的完整内部基因盒。尽管 NL04 仅引起轻微的临床疾病,且实验感染的无特定病原体 (SPF) 鸡的存活率为 100%,但观察到显着的呼吸道组织病理学损伤,揭示实验室结果与现场报告的严重 死亡率 之间存在显着的表型差异,这可能是由复杂的商业农场风险因素驱动的。SPF 鸡的组织病毒载量在鼻甲和哈德氏腺中达到峰值,证实了其主要的呼吸道趋向性。在 BALB/c 小鼠中,NL04 表现出有限的鼻腔复制,表明哺乳动物适应潜力较低。我们进一步评估了基于 NL04 的灭活 疫苗 候选药物。疫苗 诱导强大的体液免疫,减少同源攻击后的病毒脱落,并引发针对异源 H6N1 分离株的交叉中和抗体。我们的研究结果强调了这种独特的 H6N2 重配病毒的兽医相关性,并强调了在家禽中连续 基因组监测 预防新出现的禽流感威胁的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在表征一种新型 H6N2 禽流感 重配病毒,并评估其通过 疫苗接种 诱导免疫的潜力,满足了解新出现的 禽流感 威胁及其对家禽健康影响的需要。
设计与对象
该研究从中国河南的一只鸡 暴发 中分离出 H6N2 禽流感 病毒,并分析其在无特定病原体 (SPF) 鸡和 BALB/c 小鼠中的特征,重点研究其致病性和 疫苗 诱导的免疫。
核心方法
采用基因组测序确认病毒组成,并实验感染SPF鸡以评估临床疾病和组织病理学损伤。BALB/c 小鼠还接受了鼻复制测试,以评估哺乳动物的适应潜力。
主要结果
该病毒引起 SPF 鸡的轻微临床疾病和 100% 存活率,但观察到显着的呼吸道组织病理学损伤。在 BALB/c 小鼠中,它表现出有限的鼻腔复制,表明哺乳动物的适应潜力较低。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了实验室结果与现场报告的严重程度之间的表型差异,可能受到商业农场风险因素的影响,并强调了 基因组监测 对于新兴 禽流感 威胁的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了这种 H6N2 重配病毒的兽医相关性,并强调需要对家禽进行连续基因组监测,以减轻潜在的人畜共患病风险并改进疾病预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据仅限于实验室环境,观察到的表型差异可能受到实验中未控制的环境因素的影响,导致结论可信度中等。

Characterization and vaccine-induced immunity of a novel H6N2 avian influenza reassortant virus with H9N2-derived NA and internal genes from poultry in Henan, China

Authors: Lin Liu, Yu-Man Liu, Xiao-Jie Zhou, Hang Liao, Xue Pei, Yu-Feng Ren, Wen-Wen Zhou, Wen-Ming Gao, Jing-Rui Liu, Jing-Jing Chang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract In December 2025, an H6N2 avian influenza virus (A/chicken/Henan/NL04/2025, NL04) was isolated from a chicken outbreak in Henan, China with 15% field mortality and severe respiratory disease. Genome sequencing confirmed it is a novel reassortant possessing a waterfowl‐origin H6‐derived HA, H9N2‐derived NA, and the complete internal‐gene cassette of the H9N2 G57 genotype. Although NL04 caused only mild clinical disease and 100% survival in experimentally infected specific-pathogen-free (SPF) chickens, prominent respiratory‐tract histopathological damage was observed, revealing a marked phenotypic discrepancy between laboratory outcomes and severe field‐reported mortality that is likely driven by complex commercial‐farm risk factors. Tissue viral loads in SPF chickens peaked in nasal turbinates and the Harderian gland, confirming its predominant respiratory tropism. In BALB/c mice, NL04 exhibited limited nasal replication, indicating low mammalian‐adaptation potential. We further evaluated an inactivated vaccine candidate based on NL04. The vaccine induced robust humoral immunity, reduced viral shedding after homologous challenge, and elicited cross‐neutralizing antibodies against a heterologous H6N1 isolate. Our findings highlight the veterinary relevance of this unique H6N2 reassortant and stress the value of continuous genomic surveillance among poultry to prevent emerging avian‐influenza threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterize a novel H6N2 avian influenza reassortant virus and assess its potential to induce immunity through vaccination, addressing the need to understand emerging avian influenza threats and their implications for poultry health.
Design and population
The study involved isolating the H6N2 avian influenza virus from a chicken outbreak in Henan, China, and analyzing its characteristics in specific-pathogen-free (SPF) chickens and BALB/c mice, with a focus on its pathogenicity and vaccine-induced immunity.
Core methods
Genome sequencing was used to confirm the viral composition, and SPF chickens were experimentally infected to evaluate clinical disease and histopathological damage. BALB/c mice were also tested for nasal replication to assess mammalian adaptation potential.
Main results
The virus caused mild clinical disease and 100% survival in SPF chickens, but significant respiratory tract histopathological damage was observed. In BALB/c mice, it showed limited nasal replication, suggesting low mammalian adaptation potential.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a phenotypic discrepancy between laboratory outcomes and field-reported severity, likely influenced by commercial farm risk factors, and highlight the importance of genomic surveillance for emerging avian influenza threats.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study underscores the veterinary relevance of this H6N2 reassortant and emphasizes the need for continuous genomic monitoring in poultry to mitigate potential zoonotic risks and improve disease prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited to laboratory settings, and the observed phenotypic differences may be influenced by environmental factors not controlled in the experiment, resulting in moderate confidence in the conclusions.

中国广东 禽流感 H14 病毒的遗传特征:洲际 传播 和东亚-澳大利亚迁飞路线重配的证据。

Genetic characterization of avian influenza H14 viruses in Guangdong, China: evidence of intercontinental transmission and reassortment in the East Asian-Australasian flyway.
作者： Jie Liu, Yuanguo Li, Ruo Huang, Qiucheng Yao, Qiong Yang, Xuefeng Shen, Ye Ge, Yuwei Gao
摘要中文翻译H14 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 非常罕见,有关其在中国大陆传播的数据有限。本研究调查了中国南方 H14 病毒的生态起源和人畜共患潜力。通过全 基因组 测序和系统发育分析,以及对禽类和人型受体结合、禽类、哺乳动物和人细胞系的体外复制以及小鼠体内致病性的评估,对分离株进行了表征。分离出四种 H14 AIV(一种 H14N6、两种 H14N7 和一种 H14N8)。我们发现了一个新的 H14 传播 网络,涉及 Mareca penelope 和各种滨鸟物种,表明多宿主传播机制。广东分离株在欧亚谱系中形成了一个独特的分支,与俄罗斯和柬埔寨菌株密切相关。分离株(SZ3742/H14N6、ZJ3523/H14N7、SZ3536/H14N8)对禽类(α-2,3)和人类(α-2,6)唾液酸表现出双重受体结合特异性。人 A549 细胞中的菌株依赖性复制显示,SZ3742/H14N6 的峰值与 DF-1 细胞相当,但低于 MDCK 细胞,而其他细胞的峰值滴度较低。所有分离株在小鼠肺部以低滴度短暂复制,致病性有限。分子表征揭示了哺乳动物适应标记和典型的 HA 裂解位点基序 (PDKQTR↓GLF)。这项研究首次提供了 H14 AIV 在广东地区循环的证据,强调了东亚 - 澳大利亚迁飞路线中加强 监测 的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H14 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 非常罕见,有关其在中国大陆传播的数据有限。本研究调查了中国南方 H14 病毒的生态起源和人畜共患潜力。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注中国南部(特别是广东)的 禽流感 病毒,并检查了它们在东亚-澳大利亚迁飞路线中的 传播 和重配模式。
核心方法
通过全 基因组 测序和系统发育分析,以及对禽类和人型受体结合、禽类、哺乳动物和人细胞系的体外复制以及小鼠体内致病性的评估,对分离株进行了表征。
主要结果
分离出四种 H14 AIV(一种 H14N6、两种 H14N7 和一种 H14N8)。我们发现了一个新的 H14 传播 网络,涉及 Mareca penelope 和各种滨鸟物种,表明多宿主传播机制。人 A549 细胞中的菌株依赖性复制显示,SZ3742/H14N6 的峰值与 DF-1 细胞相当,但低于 MDCK 细胞,而其他细胞的峰值滴度较低。
解释与创新
该研究首次提供了 H14 AIV 在广东省传播的证据,强调了在东亚-澳大利亚迁飞路线中加强 监测 的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究提供了对 H14 AIV 生态动态的重要见解,可以为东亚-澳大利亚迁飞路线的针对性 监测 和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对单一地理区域的关注以及体外和体内模型的使用而受到限制,这可能无法完全代表自然的 传播 动力学。由于分离株数量有限且缺乏直接的人类感染数据,证据强度中等。

Genetic characterization of avian influenza H14 viruses in Guangdong, China: evidence of intercontinental transmission and reassortment in the East Asian-Australasian flyway.

Authors: Jie Liu, Yuanguo Li, Ruo Huang, Qiucheng Yao, Qiong Yang, Xuefeng Shen, Ye Ge, Yuwei Gao
Original abstract (English)
H14 avian influenza viruses (AIVs) are rare, with limited data on their circulation in mainland China. This study investigated the ecological origins and zoonotic potential of H14 viruses in southern China. The isolates were characterised by full-genome sequencing and phylogenetic analysis, together with assessment of avian- and human-type receptor binding, in vitro replication in avian, mammalian, and human cell lines, and in vivo pathogenicity in mice. Four H14 AIVs (one H14N6, two H14N7, and one H14N8) were isolated. We identified a novel H14 transmission network involving Mareca penelope and various shorebird species, indicating a multihost dispersal mechanism. Guangdong isolates formed a distinct subclade within the Eurasian lineage, closely related to Russian and Cambodian strains. The isolates (SZ3742/H14N6, ZJ3523/H14N7, SZ3536/H14N8) exhibited dual receptor-binding specificity for avian (α-2,3) and human (α-2,6) sialic acids. Strain-dependent replication in human A549 cells showed SZ3742/H14N6 peaked comparably to DF-1 cells but lower than MDCK cells, while the others had lower peak titres. All isolates replicated transiently at low titres in mouse lungs with limited pathogenicity. Molecular characterisation revealed mammalian adaptation markers and a typical HA cleavage site motif (PDKQTR↓GLF). This study presents the first evidence of H14 AIV circulation in Guangdong, emphasising the need for enhanced surveillance in the East Asian -Australasian Flyway.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
H14 avian influenza viruses (AIVs) are rare, with limited data on their circulation in mainland China. This study investigated the ecological origins and zoonotic potential of H14 viruses in southern China.
Design and population
The study focused on avian influenza viruses in southern China, specifically in Guangdong, and examined their transmission and reassortment patterns within the East Asian-Australasian flyway.
Core methods
The isolates were characterised by full-genome sequencing and phylogenetic analysis, together with assessment of avian- and human-type receptor binding, in vitro replication in avian, mammalian, and human cell lines, and in vivo pathogenicity in mice.
Main results
Four H14 AIVs (one H14N6, two H14N7, and one H14N8) were isolated. We identified a novel H14 transmission network involving Mareca penelope and various shorebird species, indicating a multihost dispersal mechanism. Strain-dependent replication in human A549 cells showed SZ3742/H14N6 peaked comparably to DF-1 cells but lower than MDCK cells, while the others had lower peak titres.
Interpretation and novelty
The study presents the first evidence of H14 AIV circulation in Guangdong, highlighting the need for enhanced surveillance in the East Asian-Australasian Flyway.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study provides critical insights into the ecological dynamics of H14 AIVs, which can inform targeted surveillance and prevention strategies in the East Asian-Australasian Flyway.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single geographic region and the use of in vitro and in vivo models, which may not fully represent natural transmission dynamics. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the limited number of isolates and the absence of direct human infection data.

家禽和牲畜疾病 监测、诊断 和控制

Poultry and Livestock Disease Surveillance, Diagnosis, and Control
作者： Ahmad Jamal
摘要中文翻译摘要 家禽和反刍动物的传染病限制生产、扰乱贸易并威胁人类健康。过去十年,H5N1 禽流感 及其 跨物种溢出 传染给了奶牛,口蹄疫 (FMD)、小反刍兽疫 (PPR) 和块状皮肤病也入侵了欧洲。这篇批判性综述比较了 2015 年至 2026 年发表的文献中家禽(禽流感、新城疫、马立克氏病、球虫病、鸡痘)和反刍动物(口蹄疫、布氏菌病、牛结核病、小反刍兽疫、块状皮肤病、蓝舌病)重点疾病的诊断、监测 和预防方法,覆盖全球并关注低收入和中等收入国家。使用五个明确的标准对疾病进行优先排序。综合结果表明,测试性能取决于目的、样本和 患病率,并且与测定化学相比,访问和物流更频繁地限制 诊断。疫苗 可以重塑病原体种群,正如马立克氏漏病 疫苗 和 FMD 携带动物所说明的那样,因此 疫苗接种 需要匹配监测。经济估计依赖于不相容的方法,H5N1 在牛中出现后需要跨物种 监测,而抗生素管理对于家禽至关重要。我们针对资源有限的环境提出了分层的 监测 诊断干预工作流程,并列出了研究重点,包括快速和基因组工具的现场验证以及统一的经济框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决传染病对家禽和反刍动物生产、贸易和人类健康的影响,强调需要改进 监测 和控制策略。
设计与对象
该研究对家禽和反刍动物重点疾病的诊断、监测 和预防方法进行了严格审查,重点关注 2015 年至 2026 年间发表的文献,覆盖全球并关注低收入和中等收入国家。
核心方法
该综合比较了跨疾病的诊断、监测 和预防方法,强调了测试性能如何受到目的、样本类型和 患病率 的影响,以及获取和物流如何比分析化学对 诊断 的影响更大。
主要结果
该审查确定了疾病优先顺序的五个明确标准,并强调了基于这些因素的诊断有效性的可变性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,疫苗 可以改变病原体种群,正如马立克氏病和 FMD 所见,并且 疫苗接种 策略必须伴随适当的监测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要量身定制的 监测 和诊断方法以及统一的经济框架,以在资源有限的环境中有效管理 禽流感 和相关疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对现有文献的依赖以及经济估计方法潜在的不兼容而受到限制,但由于其全面的全球覆盖范围和对低收入和中等收入国家的关注,证据是强有力的。

Poultry and Livestock Disease Surveillance, Diagnosis, and Control

Authors: Ahmad Jamal
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Infectious diseases of poultry and ruminants limit production, disrupt trade and threaten human health. The last decade brought panzootic H5N1 avian influenza, its spillover into dairy cattle, and incursions of foot-and-mouth disease (FMD), peste des petits ruminants (PPR) and lumpy skin disease into Europe. This critical review compares diagnostic, surveillance and prevention approaches for priority diseases of poultry (avian influenza, Newcastle disease, Marek's disease, coccidiosis, fowlpox) and ruminants (FMD, brucellosis, bovine tuberculosis, PPR, lumpy skin disease, bluetongue) in literature published between 2015 and 2026, with global coverage and attention to low- and middle-income countries. Diseases were prioritised using five explicit criteria. The synthesis shows that test performance depends on purpose, specimen and prevalence, and that access and logistics limit diagnosis more often than assay chemistry. Vaccines can reshape pathogen populations, as leaky Marek's disease vaccines and FMD carrier animals illustrate, so vaccination needs matched monitoring. Economic estimates rest on incompatible methods, cross-species surveillance is needed after H5N1 emerged in cattle, and antimicrobial stewardship is essential in poultry. We propose a tiered surveillance–diagnostic–intervention workflow for resource-limited settings and list research priorities, including field validation of rapid and genomic tools and harmonised economic frameworks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the impact of infectious diseases on poultry and ruminant production, trade and human health, highlighting the need for improved surveillance and control strategies.
Design and population
The study is a critical review of diagnostic, surveillance and prevention approaches for priority diseases in poultry and ruminants, focusing on literature published between 2015 and 2026 with global coverage and attention to low- and middle-income countries.
Core methods
The synthesis compares diagnostic, surveillance and prevention methods across diseases, emphasizing how test performance is influenced by purpose, specimen type and prevalence, and how access and logistics affect diagnosis more than assay chemistry.
Main results
The review identified five explicit criteria for disease prioritization and highlighted the variability in diagnostic effectiveness based on these factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that vaccines can alter pathogen populations, as seen with Marek's disease and FMD, and that vaccination strategies must be accompanied by appropriate monitoring.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the need for tailored surveillance and diagnostic approaches, as well as harmonized economic frameworks, to effectively manage avian influenza and related diseases in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on existing literature and the potential incompatibility of economic estimation methods, but the evidence is strong due to its comprehensive global coverage and attention to low- and middle-income countries.

优化 禽流感 从水中富集病毒 RNA:小体积样品浓缩方法的比较

Optimizing Avian Influenza Viral RNA Enrichment from Water: A Comparison of Concentration Methods for Low-Volume Samples
作者： Joaquín Neumann-Heise, Chiara Holzer, Claudia Stange, Andreas Tiehm, Albert Perlas, Lara Urban, Timm Harder, Lutz Breuer, Sascha Knauf, Anja Globig
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在优化检测少量水样中 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) RNA 的方法,因为传统方法在此类样品的灵敏度和特异性方面面临限制。
设计与对象
该研究比较了五种样品处理方法,用于改善小量(40 mL)水样(包括自来水和苦咸水)中 AIV RNA 的检测,并在模拟现场条件下添加了 H4N6 亚型 AIV。
核心方法
这些方法包括拭子采样、聚乙二醇沉淀、活性炭捕获、活性炭捕获和聚乙二醇沉淀组合(AC/PEG)以及负电膜吸附(EM)。随后的测试评估了不同温度和持续时间下的储存条件。
主要结果
根据 RT-qPCR 结果,AC/PEG 和 EM 显示出最强的整体性能,尽管它们的相对性能因实验条件而异。自来水中 qPCR 衍生的病毒信号通常高于微咸水中,而小麦的存在与较低的信号相关,特别是对于拭子采样。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,AC/PEG 和 EM 可有效改善少量水样中的 AIV RNA 检测,其中 AC/PEG 是 EM 的经济高效替代品。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持使用病毒富集方法来增强低体积水样中AIV的检测和表征,这可以改进禽流感的环境监测策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于模拟现场条件和单一 AIV 亚型,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于受控环境和缺乏现场验证,证据是中等的。

Optimizing Avian Influenza Viral RNA Enrichment from Water: A Comparison of Concentration Methods for Low-Volume Samples

Authors: Joaquín Neumann-Heise, Chiara Holzer, Claudia Stange, Andreas Tiehm, Albert Perlas, Lara Urban, Timm Harder, Lutz Breuer, Sascha Knauf, Anja Globig
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Feed-baited water bins may provide a complementary approach for avian influenza virus (AIV) surveillance in wild waterbird populations. In a previous study, water from these bins was sampled using swabs after exposure to visiting birds, but the low sample volume limited the detection and subsequent characterization of AIV RNA (F. Homeier-Bachmann, L. Globig, One Health 23:101548, 2026, https://doi.org/10.1016/j.onehlt.2026.101548). Here, we compared five sample-processing methods for improving AIV RNA detection in small (40-mL) water samples (tap and brackish water) spiked with an AIV of the H4N6 subtype under simulated field conditions: swab sampling, polyethylene glycol precipitation, activated carbon capture, combined activated carbon capture and polyethylene glycol precipitation (AC/PEG), and electronegative membrane adsorption (EM). AC/PEG and EM showed the strongest overall performance based on RT-qPCR results, although their relative performance varied across experimental conditions. The qPCR-derived viral signal was generally higher in tap water than in brackish water, whereas wheat presence was associated with a lower signal, particularly for swab sampling. Subsequent testing was conducted to ascertain the most effective storage conditions with regard to temperature and duration. During storage, AIV RNA detection remained relatively stable at −20 °C for up to four weeks, with only minor increases in mean Ct values in both water matrices. Greater increases were observed at room temperature and 4 °C, with significantly higher Ct values at both temperatures than at −20 °C in both water matrices at Weeks 2 and 4 ( p < 0.05). These findings support the potential of AC/PEG and EM to facilitate the recovery, detection and subsequent characterization of AIV from low-volume water samples by improving viral RNA detection using RT-qPCR. Given its overall performance and lower cost compared with EM, AC/PEG may represent a practical option for field-based surveillance. Regarding storage, −20 °C provided the best conditions for maintaining AIV RNA detection, with relatively stable results for up to four weeks. Importance Environmental surveillance can complement conventional approaches for detecting avian influenza virus (AIV) circulation in wild waterbirds. Feed-baited water bins offer a non-invasive sampling strategy, but processing low-volume water samples may require viral enrichment to improve AIV RNA detection. Here, we show that combined activated carbon capture and polyethylene glycol precipitation (AC/PEG) is a promising, cost-effective method for improving AIV RNA detection in low-volume water samples. These findings highlight the potential of viral enrichment methods to support the use of simple, practical and non-invasive approaches for AIV surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to optimize methods for detecting avian influenza virus (AIV) RNA in low-volume water samples, as conventional approaches face limitations in sensitivity and specificity for such samples.
Design and population
The study compared five sample-processing methods for improving AIV RNA detection in small (40-mL) water samples, including tap and brackish water, spiked with an H4N6 subtype AIV under simulated field conditions.
Core methods
The methods included swab sampling, polyethylene glycol precipitation, activated carbon capture, combined activated carbon capture and polyethylene glycol precipitation (AC/PEG), and electronegative membrane adsorption (EM). Subsequent testing evaluated storage conditions at different temperatures and durations.
Main results
AC/PEG and EM showed the strongest overall performance based on RT-qPCR results, although their relative performance varied across experimental conditions. The qPCR-derived viral signal was generally higher in tap water than in brackish water, whereas wheat presence was associated with a lower signal, particularly for swab sampling.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that AC/PEG and EM are effective for improving AIV RNA detection in low-volume water samples, with AC/PEG being a cost-effective alternative to EM.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results support the use of viral enrichment methods to enhance the detection and characterization of AIV in low-volume water samples, which can improve environmental surveillance strategies for avian influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to simulated field conditions and a single AIV subtype, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate due to the controlled environment and lack of field validation.

医护人员中已存在针对 H5Nx 的跨亚型血清学反应

Pre-existing cross-subtype serological responses against H5Nx in health care workers
作者： Katrina Dionne, Étienne Bélanger, Mehdi Benlarbi, Guillaume Beaudoin-Bussières, Claudia Santos Saenz, Shilei Ding, Catherine Bourassa, Halima Medjahed, Morgan Jennings, Geneviève Laroche
摘要中文翻译摘要 自 2023 年以来,据报道人类 高致病性禽流感 A (H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 病例有所增加。尽管季节性流感 高致病性禽流感 (H1N1/H3N2) 被证明可有效对抗地方性甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的再感染病例,但其引发针对 (H5N1) 交叉反应抗体的能力仍不清楚。在这里,我们使用 2024 年流感季节性 疫苗接种 期间加拿大蒙特利尔 CRCHUM 卫生保健工作者 队列研究 的血浆样本测量了 疫苗 引发的针对季节性 (H1N1/H3N2) 和各种禽类 A 亚型 (H5Nx) 的抗体反应。测量了它们的血浆识别十种流感病毒株的血凝素(HA)和神经氨酸酶(NA)抗原的能力。测量了它们的中和活性,以及​​它们介导针对稳定表达 HA 和 NA 的细胞的抗体依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC) 的能力。总体而言,针对 H1N1/H3N2 的季节性 疫苗接种 引发了能够识别多种 HA 和 NA 的抗体,并且在某些个体中,这转化为中和和 ADCC 活性的增加,包括针对 H5Nx 的抗体。这项研究提供了有关 疫苗接种 如何形成针对禽类 H5Nx 的交叉反应抗体反应的深刻信息。重要性 甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 在人群中流行,每年导致数十万人死亡,有时还会导致流感大流行。自 2023 年以来,人类感染禽类 IAV 的数量有所增加,并报告了一些死亡病例,其中大部分与 H5N1 亚型分支 2.3.4.4b 有关。季节性 疫苗 对 IAV 有效,但其对禽 IAV 的功效尚不清楚。在这里,在成人 队列研究 中出现季节性流感 队列研究 后,我们报告了高度异质性的血清学反应,可能是由于预先存在的免疫力和 HA 序列相似性。这些发现提供了对预先存在的和 疫苗 诱导的针对 H5 流感病毒的免疫力的广度的深入了解,并可能为 疫苗接种 策略的开发提供信息,该策略旨在针对新出现的流感病毒提供更广泛的保护。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查季节性 疫苗接种 针对 H1N1/H3N2 病毒后医护人员针对 H5Nx 的跨亚型血清学反应,解决季节性 疫苗 对 禽流感 病毒功效的不确定性。
设计与对象
该研究测量了 2024 年流感 疫苗接种 季节期间加拿大蒙特利尔 队列研究 医护人员血浆样本中 疫苗 引发的针对季节性 (H1N1/H3N2) 和禽 (H5Nx) 流感病毒的抗体反应。
核心方法
测试了血浆样本识别十种流感病毒株的血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 抗原的能力,并测量了它们的中和活性和抗体依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC)。
主要结果
季节性 疫苗接种 引发识别多种 HA 和 NA 抗原的抗体,在某些个体中,这转化为中和和 ADCC 活性增加,包括针对 H5Nx 的活性。
解释与创新
该研究深入了解了 疫苗接种 如何形成针对禽类 H5Nx 的交叉反应抗体反应,表明预先存在的免疫力和 HA 序列相似性在血清学反应中发挥着作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为 疫苗接种 战略的制定提供信息,该战略旨在针对新出现的流感病毒,特别是禽类 H5Nx 提供更广泛的保护。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性,包括血清学反应的异质性和预先存在的免疫力的潜在影响,可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

Pre-existing cross-subtype serological responses against H5Nx in health care workers

Authors: Katrina Dionne, Étienne Bélanger, Mehdi Benlarbi, Guillaume Beaudoin-Bussières, Claudia Santos Saenz, Shilei Ding, Catherine Bourassa, Halima Medjahed, Morgan Jennings, Geneviève Laroche
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT A rise in cases of highly pathogenic avian influenza A (H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b in humans has been reported since 2023. Although influenza seasonal vaccination (H1N1/H3N2) was shown to be effective against cases of reinfection with endemic influenza A viruses (IAVs), its capacity to elicit cross-reactive antibodies against (H5N1) remains unclear. Here, we measured vaccine-elicited antibody responses against seasonal (H1N1/H3N2) and various avian subtypes A (H5Nx) using plasma samples from the CRCHUM Health Care Workers cohort in Montreal, Canada, during the 2024 influenza seasonal vaccination. The capacity of their plasma to recognize hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) antigens from ten influenza strains was measured. Their neutralizing activity, as well as their capacity to mediate antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity (ADCC) against cells stably expressing HA and NA, was measured. Overall, seasonal vaccination against H1N1/H3N2 elicited antibodies able to recognize several HA and NA, and in some individuals, this translated to increased neutralization and ADCC activity, including against H5Nx. This study provides insightful information on how vaccination shapes cross-reactive antibody responses against avian H5Nx. IMPORTANCE Influenza A viruses (IAVs) are endemic in the human population and responsible for hundreds of thousands of deaths every year, occasionally leading to influenza pandemics. Since 2023, there has been a rise in avian IAV infections in humans, with some deaths reported, mostly related to the H5N1 subtype clade 2.3.4.4b. Seasonal vaccines against IAVs are effective, but their efficacy against avian IAVs is not well known. Here, following influenza seasonal vaccination in a cohort of adults, we report highly heterogeneous serological responses, possibly due to pre-existing immunity and HA sequence similarity. These findings provide insight into the breadth of pre-existing and vaccine-induced immunity against H5 influenza viruses and may inform the development of vaccination strategies aimed at providing broader protection against emerging influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the cross-subtype serological responses against H5Nx in health care workers following seasonal vaccination against H1N1/H3N2, addressing the uncertainty regarding the efficacy of seasonal vaccines against avian influenza viruses.
Design and population
The study measured vaccine-elicited antibody responses against seasonal (H1N1/H3N2) and avian (H5Nx) influenza viruses in plasma samples from a cohort of health care workers in Montreal, Canada, during the 2024 influenza vaccination season.
Core methods
Plasma samples were tested for their ability to recognize hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) antigens from ten influenza strains, and their neutralizing activity and antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity (ADCC) were measured.
Main results
Seasonal vaccination elicited antibodies that recognized several HA and NA antigens, and in some individuals, this translated to increased neutralization and ADCC activity, including against H5Nx.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides insight into how vaccination shapes cross-reactive antibody responses against avian H5Nx, suggesting that pre-existing immunity and HA sequence similarity play a role in serological responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the development of vaccination strategies aimed at providing broader protection against emerging influenza viruses, particularly avian H5Nx.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations, including the heterogeneity of serological responses and the potential influence of pre-existing immunity, may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate.

牛粘病毒抗性蛋白 (Mx)1(但不是 Mx2)介导 抗病毒药物 针对人类和 禽流感 A 病毒(包括 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b)的活性

Bovine myxovirus resistance protein (Mx)1, but not Mx2, mediates antiviral activity against human and avian influenza A viruses, including H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b
作者： Rubaiyea Farrukee, Yongyan Xia, Rachel Morris, James B. Barnes, Clyde Dapat, Saira Hussain, Yi-Mo Deng, Victoria Meliopoulos, Stacey Schultz-Cherry, Sarah L. Londrigan
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒最近感染了美国的奶牛,引起了对人类 跨物种溢出 的担忧。牛 宿主 防御因子(包括粘病毒抗性蛋白(bMx1 和 bMx2))的 抗病毒药物 作用仍相对未得到充分研究。我们检测了 HPAI H5N1 感染后分化牛细胞中 bMx1/bMx2 的诱导,并使用过表达、敲低和诱变方法评估了它们的 抗病毒药物 活性。bMx1 和 bMx2 在乳腺细胞中的诱导作用比在气管细胞中的诱导作用更强。bMx1 而非 bMx2 的过表达可显着抑制季节性甲型流感病毒 (IAV)、低致病性和 高致病性禽流感 病毒,包括 H5N1 2.3.4.4b,但不能抑制乙型流感病毒。相反,bMx1 的敲低导致 IAV 生长增强。此外,bMx1 抑制病毒复制的早期阶段,从而减少病毒转录和病毒复制周期的后续步骤。已知的 突变 可以降低人类 MxA (hMxA) 活性,同时也会损害 bMx1 功能,而与 hMxA 抗性相关的病毒核蛋白 (NP) 中的 突变 也会降低病毒对 bMx1 的敏感性。此外,系统发育分析表明,一小部分(>0.03%)禽类 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 病毒已经携带其中一些 NP 突变。这些发现表明,虽然当前的 H5N1 病毒在很大程度上仍然对 bMx1 敏感,但病毒 进化 或机会性重配可能会导致 Mx 耐药性并增加大流行风险。重要性 高致病性禽流感 病毒已扩散到奶牛中,并自 2024 年以来一直在美国奶牛场传播。奶牛对流感病毒的先天 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 尚未得到广泛研究。在这项研究中,我们重点关注 Mx 蛋白,它是针对病毒的先天免疫防御系统的关键部分。我们专门研究了牛 Mx 蛋白,并证明了 抗病毒药物 对不同流感病毒(包括高致病性毒株)的活性。我们还表明,牛 Mx1 的 抗病毒药物 活性机制与人类等效的 MxA 蛋白非常相似。此外,我们发现这些禽类病毒中的一小部分具有与牛 Mx1 和人 MxA 蛋白抗性相关的 突变。总体而言,这项工作强调了高致病性流感病毒通过在牛(一种与人类关系密切的哺乳动物物种)中持续传播而增加的大流行风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒最近感染了美国的奶牛,引起了对人类 跨物种溢出 的担忧。牛 宿主 防御因子(包括粘病毒抗性蛋白(bMx1 和 bMx2))的 抗病毒药物 作用仍相对未得到充分研究。
设计与对象
我们检测了 HPAI H5N1 感染后分化牛细胞中 bMx1/bMx2 的诱导,并使用过表达、敲低和诱变方法评估了它们的 抗病毒药物 活性。bMx1 和 bMx2 在乳腺细胞中的诱导作用比在气管细胞中的诱导作用更强。
核心方法
我们专门研究了牛 Mx 蛋白,并证明了 抗病毒药物 对不同流感病毒(包括高致病性毒株)的活性。已知的 突变 可以降低人类 MxA (hMxA) 活性,同时也会损害 bMx1 功能,而与 hMxA 抗性相关的病毒核蛋白 (NP) 中的 突变 也会降低病毒对 bMx1 的敏感性。
主要结果
bMx1 而非 bMx2 的过表达可显着抑制季节性甲型流感病毒 (IAV)、低致病性和 高致病性禽流感 病毒,包括 H5N1 2.3.4.4b,但不能抑制乙型流感病毒。bMx1 的敲低导致 IAV 生长增强。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,虽然当前的 H5N1 病毒在很大程度上仍然对 bMx1 敏感,但病毒 进化 或机会性重配可能会导致 Mx 耐药性并增加大流行风险。此外,我们发现这些禽类病毒中的一小部分具有与牛 Mx1 和人 MxA 蛋白抗性相关的 突变。
科研与公卫意义
总体而言,这项工作强调了高致病性流感病毒通过在牛(一种与人类关系密切的哺乳动物物种)中持续传播而增加的大流行风险。牛对流感病毒的先天性 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 尚未得到广泛研究。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究的局限性或证据强度。这些发现基于牛细胞的实验方法,表明对病毒耐药性和大流行风险的潜在影响。

Bovine myxovirus resistance protein (Mx)1, but not Mx2, mediates antiviral activity against human and avian influenza A viruses, including H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b

Authors: Rubaiyea Farrukee, Yongyan Xia, Rachel Morris, James B. Barnes, Clyde Dapat, Saira Hussain, Yi-Mo Deng, Victoria Meliopoulos, Stacey Schultz-Cherry, Sarah L. Londrigan
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have recently infected dairy cows in the United States, raising concerns about human spillover. The antiviral roles of bovine host defense factors, including myxovirus resistance proteins (bMx1 and bMx2), remain relatively understudied. We examined bMx1/bMx2 induction in differentiated bovine cells following HPAI H5N1 infection and assessed their antiviral activity using overexpression, knockdown, and mutagenesis approaches. Both bMx1 and bMx2 were more strongly induced in mammary cells than in tracheal cells. Overexpression of bMx1, but not bMx2, significantly inhibited seasonal influenza A viruses (IAVs), low-pathogenic, and highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses, including H5N1 2.3.4.4b, but not influenza B viruses. Conversely, knockdown of bMx1 resulted in enhanced IAV growth. Further, bMx1 inhibited early stages of viral replication, acting to reduce viral transcription and subsequent steps in the viral replication cycle. Known mutations that reduce human MxA (hMxA) activity also impaired bMx1 function, and mutations in the viral nucleoprotein (NP) associated with resistance to hMxA also reduced virus sensitivity to bMx1. Furthermore, phylogenetic analysis showed that a small minority (>0.03%) of avian H5N1 2.3.4.4b viruses already carry some of these NP mutations. These findings suggest that while current H5N1 viruses largely remain susceptible to bMx1, viral evolution or opportunistic reassortment could drive Mx resistance and increase pandemic risk. IMPORTANCE Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses have spilled over into dairy cows and have been circulating on dairy farms in the United States since 2024. The innate antiviral immune responses of cows to influenza viruses have not been extensively studied. In this study, we focused on Mx proteins, a key part of the innate immune defense system toward viruses. We specifically looked at bovine Mx proteins and demonstrated antiviral activity toward different influenza viruses, including the highly pathogenic strains. We also showed that the mechanism of antiviral activity of bovine Mx1 was very similar to that of the human equivalent MxA protein. Further, we found that a small minority of these avian viruses have mutations associated with resistance to both bovine Mx1 and human MxA proteins. Overall, this work highlights the elevated pandemic risk for highly pathogenic influenza viruses through continual circulation in cows, a mammalian species in close proximity to humans.

Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have recently infected dairy cows in the United States, raising concerns about human spillover. The antiviral roles of bovine host defense factors, including myxovirus resistance proteins (bMx1 and bMx2), remain relatively understudied.

We examined bMx1/bMx2 induction in differentiated bovine cells following HPAI H5N1 infection and assessed their antiviral activity using overexpression, knockdown, and mutagenesis approaches. Both bMx1 and bMx2 were more strongly induced in mammary cells than in tracheal cells.
Core methods
We specifically looked at bovine Mx proteins and demonstrated antiviral activity toward different influenza viruses, including the highly pathogenic strains. Known mutations that reduce human MxA (hMxA) activity also impaired bMx1 function, and mutations in the viral nucleoprotein (NP) associated with resistance to hMxA also reduced virus sensitivity to bMx1.
Main results
Overexpression of bMx1, but not bMx2, significantly inhibited seasonal influenza A viruses (IAVs), low-pathogenic, and highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses, including H5N1 2.3.4.4b, but not influenza B viruses. Knockdown of bMx1 resulted in enhanced IAV growth.

Further, we found that a small minority of these avian viruses have mutations associated with resistance to both bovine Mx1 and human MxA proteins.
Scientific and public-health significance
Overall, this work highlights the elevated pandemic risk for highly pathogenic influenza viruses through continual circulation in cows, a mammalian species in close proximity to humans. The innate antiviral immune responses of cows to influenza viruses have not been extensively studied.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength for the study. The findings are based on experimental approaches in bovine cells and suggest potential implications for viral resistance and pandemic risk.

表达 H5N8 HA 的重组新城疫病毒对肉鸡中 H5N1 禽流感 病毒(进化枝 2.3.4.4b)的功效

Efficacy of a Recombinant Newcastle Disease Virus Expressing H5N8 HA Against H5N1 Avian Influenza Virus (Clade 2.3.4.4b) in Broiler Chickens
作者： Katherine I. Calderón, Aldo Rojas-Neyra, Brigith Carbajal-Lévano, Gloria Guerrero-Fonseca, Ángela Montalván-Ávalos, Doris Villanueva-Pérez, Manolo Fernández-Sánchez, Manolo Fernández‐Díaz, Angela Montalván-Avalos, Manolo Fernández-Díaz
摘要中文翻译背景:自 2022 年 11 月以来,高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒亚型 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 导致秘鲁 野生鸟类 和家禽中 死亡率 较高。疫苗,例如表达禽流感病毒(AIV)血凝素(HA)抗原的重组新城疫病毒(rNDV)载体疫苗,被认为可有效预防AI;然而,疫苗中的HA蛋白必须与野外病毒抗原匹配,以防止死亡率和AI引起的病毒脱落。在这项研究中,我们开发了一种活 rNDV 载体 疫苗,其表达的 HA 蛋白与外地循环 AIV 的 HA 具有抗原相似性。方法:以rNDV rLS1毒株(亲本病毒)为病毒媒介,通过反向遗传学开发rLS1-HAOpti 疫苗(表达H5N8进化枝2.3.4.4b的HA蛋白的rNDV)。在肉鸡中评估了活 疫苗 针对 HPAI H5N1(进化枝 2.3.4.4b)攻击的免疫原性和功效。结果:疫苗 诱导显着的血清体液反应。H5N1 攻击后,疫苗 为大多数鸡提供了保护并防止病毒传播。结论:在测试的实验条件下,rNDV 疫苗 候选药物为 H5N1 攻击的肉鸡提供了针对 死亡率 和临床疾病的完全保护。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种表达 H5N8 分支 2.3.4.4b 血凝素 (HA) 蛋白的活重组新城疫病毒 (rNDV) 载体 疫苗,以预防肉鸡中由 HPAI H5N1(分支 2.3.4.4b)引起的 死亡率 和病毒脱落,解决与有效 疫苗 相匹配的需求。流行的野病毒的抗原谱。
设计与对象
该研究评估了rNDV rLS1-HAOpti 疫苗在实验条件下对受到H5N1(进化枝2.3.4.4b)攻击的肉鸡的免疫原性和功效,涉及表达H5N8进化枝2.3.4.4b的HA蛋白的活rNDV载体疫苗。
核心方法
rNDV rLS1株被用作病毒媒介,通过反向遗传学开发rLS1-HAOpti 疫苗。通过评估 H5N1(进化枝 2.3.4.4b)攻击的肉鸡的血清体液反应和防止病毒脱落来评估 疫苗 的功效。
主要结果
疫苗 诱导显着的血清体液反应,在 H5N1 攻击后,它为大多数鸡提供保护并防止病毒脱落。
解释与创新
rNDV 疫苗 候选药物在测试的实验条件下为受到 H5N1 攻击的肉鸡提供了针对 死亡率 和临床疾病的完全保护,证明了 疫苗 在家禽中预防 HPAI H5N1 的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,rNDV 疫苗 可能成为在家禽中预防 HPAI H5N1 的宝贵工具,通过降低病毒 传播 对人类的风险,为 禽流感 控制和公共卫生做出贡献。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用特定的实验挑战以及需要在现场环境中进行进一步评估。由于受控实验条件,证据强度中等。

Efficacy of a Recombinant Newcastle Disease Virus Expressing H5N8 HA Against H5N1 Avian Influenza Virus (Clade 2.3.4.4b) in Broiler Chickens

Authors: Katherine I. Calderón, Aldo Rojas-Neyra, Brigith Carbajal-Lévano, Gloria Guerrero-Fonseca, Ángela Montalván-Ávalos, Doris Villanueva-Pérez, Manolo Fernández-Sánchez, Manolo Fernández‐Díaz, Angela Montalván-Avalos, Manolo Fernández-Díaz
Original abstract (English)
Background: Since November 2022, the highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) virus subtype H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b has caused high mortality among wild birds and poultry in Peru. Vaccines, such as recombinant Newcastle disease virus (rNDV)-vectored vaccines expressing the hemagglutinin (HA) antigen of avian influenza virus (AIV), are considered effective for preventing AI; however, the HA protein in the vaccine must be antigenically matched to the field virus to prevent mortality and virus shedding caused by AI. In this study, we developed a live rNDV-vectored vaccine expressing an HA protein with antigenic similarity to the HA of field-circulating AIV. Methods: The rNDV rLS1 strain (the parental virus) was used as a viral vector to develop the rLS1-HAOpti vaccine (an rNDV expressing the HA protein of H5N8 clade 2.3.4.4b) through reverse genetics. The immunogenicity and efficacy of the live vaccine against an HPAI H5N1 (clade 2.3.4.4b) challenge were evaluated in broiler chickens. Results: The vaccine induced a significant serum humoral response. After the H5N1 challenge, the vaccine provided protection and prevented virus shedding in most of the chickens. Conclusions: The rNDV vaccine candidate provided complete protection against mortality and clinical disease in broiler chickens challenged with H5N1 under the tested experimental conditions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a live recombinant Newcastle disease virus (rNDV)-vectored vaccine expressing the hemagglutinin (HA) protein of H5N8 clade 2.3.4.4b to prevent mortality and virus shedding caused by HPAI H5N1 (clade 2.3.4.4b) in broiler chickens, addressing the need for effective vaccines that match the antigenic profile of circulating field viruses.
Design and population
The study evaluated the immunogenicity and efficacy of the rNDV rLS1-HAOpti vaccine in broiler chickens challenged with H5N1 (clade 2.3.4.4b) under experimental conditions, involving a live rNDV-vectored vaccine expressing the HA protein of H5N8 clade 2.3.4.4b.
Core methods
The rNDV rLS1 strain was used as a viral vector to develop the rLS1-HAOpti vaccine through reverse genetics. The vaccine's efficacy was assessed by evaluating the serum humoral response and preventing virus shedding in broiler chickens challenged with H5N1 (clade 2.3.4.4b).
Main results
The vaccine induced a significant serum humoral response, and after the H5N1 challenge, it provided protection and prevented virus shedding in most of the chickens.
Interpretation and novelty
The rNDV vaccine candidate provided complete protection against mortality and clinical disease in broiler chickens challenged with H5N1 under the tested experimental conditions, demonstrating the vaccine's potential for preventing HPAI H5N1 in poultry.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the rNDV vaccine could be a valuable tool for preventing HPAI H5N1 in poultry, contributing to avian influenza control and public health by reducing the risk of virus transmission to humans.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a specific experimental challenge and the need for further evaluation in field settings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the controlled experimental conditions.

南象海豹的种群间连通性和高致病性 传播 可能的种内 传播 途径

Inter-population connectivity of southern elephant seals and the likely intra-species transmission pathways of high pathogenicity avian influenza
作者： Clive R. McMahon, Mark Andrew Hindell, Robert G. Harcourt, Isobel Lerpiniere, Ian David Jonsen, Christophe Guinet, Rupert Woods, Marthan N. Bester, Jane L. Younger, Nicholas M. Fountain‐Jones
摘要中文翻译高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 已蔓延至鸟类以外,影响整个南大洋和亚南极地区的海豹,其中南象海豹 (Mirounga leonina) 受到的破坏尤其严重。这种病毒很可能是通过 跨物种溢出 从受感染的候鸟那里引入的,已导致其大部分活动范围内数以万计的成年海豹和幼崽死亡。我们使用来自南印度洋和太平洋的 20 年象海豹活动数据来评估海豹与海豹之间的 传播 是否可以在繁殖群体之间传播 HPAI H5N1,尽管这些群体之间相距甚远(马里恩岛、克罗泽岛、凯尔盖朗岛和麦格理岛)。海豹在冬季换羽后的旅行中,其海上分布存在大量重叠,海豹以平均 3.5 公里/小时的速度旅行数周。研究了两条 传播 途径:(1)陆地“踏脚石”路线,即受感染的海豹可以在短时间内在群体之间传播病毒以保持传染性,从马里恩岛到凯尔盖朗是可行的,但从凯尔盖朗到麦格理岛则不可行;(2) 海豹之间的海上遭遇,其发生频率足以启用 传播。研究结果表明,一旦在麦格理岛建立,该病毒可能会进一步传播到新西兰的亚南极岛屿和新西兰大陆。虽然密封到密封 传播 似乎是可能的,但我们认为这不太可能。尽管如此,了解海上接触率可以增强对 H5N1 流行病学 的了解,并证明将长期种群监测与移动数据相结合对于了解野生动物疾病动态的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 已蔓延至鸟类以外,影响整个南大洋和亚南极地区的海豹,其中南象海豹 (Mirounga leonina) 受到的破坏尤其严重。
设计与对象
这种病毒很可能是通过 跨物种溢出 从受感染的候鸟那里引入的,已导致其大部分活动范围内数以万计的成年海豹和幼崽死亡。
核心方法
我们使用来自南印度洋和太平洋的 20 年象海豹活动数据来评估海豹与海豹之间的 传播 是否可以在繁殖群体之间传播 HPAI H5N1,尽管这些群体之间相距甚远(马里恩岛、克罗泽岛、凯尔盖朗岛和麦格理岛)。
主要结果
海豹在冬季换羽后的旅行中,其海上分布存在大量重叠,海豹以平均 3.5 公里/小时的速度旅行数周。
解释与创新
研究了两条 传播 途径:(1)陆地“踏脚石”路线,即受感染的海豹可以在短时间内在群体之间传播病毒以保持传染性,从马里恩岛到凯尔盖朗是可行的,但从凯尔盖朗到麦格理岛则不可行;(2) 海豹之间的海上遭遇,其发生频率足以启用 传播。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,一旦在麦格理岛建立,该病毒可能会进一步传播到新西兰的亚南极岛屿和新西兰大陆。
局限与证据强度
虽然密封到密封 传播 似乎是可能的,但我们认为这不太可能。

Inter-population connectivity of southern elephant seals and the likely intra-species transmission pathways of high pathogenicity avian influenza

Authors: Clive R. McMahon, Mark Andrew Hindell, Robert G. Harcourt, Isobel Lerpiniere, Ian David Jonsen, Christophe Guinet, Rupert Woods, Marthan N. Bester, Jane L. Younger, Nicholas M. Fountain‐Jones
Original abstract (English)
High Pathogenicity Avian Influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b has spread beyond birds to affect seals across the Southern Ocean and sub-Antarctic region, with southern elephant seals ( Mirounga leonina ) particularly devastated. The virus, likely introduced via spillover from infected migratory birds, has killed tens of thousands of adult seals and pups throughout most of their range. We used twenty years of elephant seal movement data from the southern Indian and Pacific oceans to assess whether seal-to-seal transmission could spread HPAI H5N1 between breeding colonies, despite the vast distances separating them (Marion Island, Iles Crozet, Iles Kerguelen, and Macquarie Island). There was substantial overlap in seals’ at-sea distributions during their winter post-moult trips, when seals travel for weeks at average speeds of 3.5 km/h. Two transmission pathways were examined: (1) terrestrial “stepping stone” routes, where infected seals could pass the virus between colonies during short intervals to remain infectious were feasible from Marion Island to Kerguelen but not from Kerguelen to Macquarie Island; and (2) at-sea encounters between seals, which occurred frequently enough to enable transmission. The findings suggest that once established at Macquarie Island, the virus could potentially spread further to New Zealand’s sub-Antarctic islands and mainland New Zealand. While seal-to-seal transmission appears possible, we conclude this is unlikely. Nonetheless, understanding at-sea contact rates enhances knowledge of H5N1 epidemiology and demonstrates the value of combining long-term population monitoring with movement data to understand wildlife disease dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High Pathogenicity Avian Influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b has spread beyond birds to affect seals across the Southern Ocean and sub-Antarctic region, with southern elephant seals (Mirounga leonina) particularly devastated.
Design and population
The virus, likely introduced via spillover from infected migratory birds, has killed tens of thousands of adult seals and pups throughout most of their range.
Core methods
We used twenty years of elephant seal movement data from the southern Indian and Pacific oceans to assess whether seal-to-seal transmission could spread HPAI H5N1 between breeding colonies, despite the vast distances separating them (Marion Island, Iles Crozet, Iles Kerguelen, and Macquarie Island).
Main results
There was substantial overlap in seals’ at-sea distributions during their winter post-moult trips, when seals travel for weeks at average speeds of 3.5 km/h.
Interpretation and novelty
Two transmission pathways were examined: (1) terrestrial “stepping stone” routes, where infected seals could pass the virus between colonies during short intervals to remain infectious were feasible from Marion Island to Kerguelen but not from Kerguelen to Macquarie Island; and (2) at-sea encounters between seals, which occurred frequently enough to enable transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that once established at Macquarie Island, the virus could potentially spread further to New Zealand’s sub-Antarctic islands and mainland New Zealand.
Limitations and evidence strength
While seal-to-seal transmission appears possible, we conclude this is unlikely.

小鼠和仓鼠模型中牛和人甲型流感 H5N1 病毒的感染和 传播 动态

Infection and transmission dynamics of bovine and human influenza A H5N1 viruses in mouse and hamster models
作者： Mohammed Nooruzzaman, David W. Buchholz, Ruchi Rani, Pablo Sebastian Britto de Oliveira, Richard A. Adeleke, Chen Feng, Nicholas Vazquez, Elena A. Demeter, Hector C. Aguilar, Diego G. Diel
摘要中文翻译摘要 在这里,我们在哺乳动物模型中研究了牛源和人源 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 基因型 B3.13 病毒的 发病机制 和接触 传播。利用反向遗传学,我们拯救了三种天然存在的病毒,rTX2/24(牛源)、rTexas/37和rMichigan/90(均来自人源),并在体外和体内比较了它们与野生型牛TX2/24病毒株的感染动态、复制和致病性。所有四种病毒在四种哺乳动物细胞系中都表现出类似的复制动力学。然而,rMichigan/90 菌株在牛和人类细胞中表现出较小的斑块。体内研究表明,感染任何一种病毒的小鼠都会在 4-5 天内死亡。然而,与其他毒株相比,感染 rMichigan/90 病毒的小鼠表现出略低的病毒复制和脱落。仓鼠模型显示出比小鼠更温和的感染表型,其中所有病毒均引起体重减轻和口腔脱落,在组织中观察到强劲的病毒复制,但 rMichigan/90 病毒的复制和脱落减少。接触者 传播 对仓鼠的研究表明,这些病毒的传播能力有限,只有四分之一的动物接种了 rMichigan/90 病毒,将其传播给初次接触者。这些发现表明,牛源和人源 H5N1 基因型 B3.13 病毒在啮齿动物模型中均表现出高致病性,尽管在仓鼠模型中总体呼吸道传播率仍然较低。重要性 2024 年,甲型 H5N1 流感病毒从 野生鸟类 传播到美国的奶牛中。此后,人类感染人数不断增加,已确认至少 71 例确诊病例。在本研究中,我们评估并比较了基因型 B3.13 的牛和两种人类 H5N1 病毒的致病性和传播性。我们的结果表明,虽然所有病毒在小鼠和仓鼠模型中均具有致病性,但牛分离株 TX2/24 比人源病毒具有更广泛的组织趋向性。接触型 传播 研究表明,这些病毒在仓鼠接触型 传播 模型中传播的能力有限。这些发现强调了 H5N1 病毒在哺乳动物中的高致病性,并表明其呼吸道传播潜力在仓鼠模型中仍然较低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在在哺乳动物模型中研究牛源和人源 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 基因型 B3.13 病毒的 发病机制 和接触 传播。背景突出显示H5N1病毒跨物种溢出在2024年从野生鸟类进入美国奶牛,导致人类感染数量增加。
设计与对象
该研究利用反向遗传学拯救了三种天然存在的病毒:rTX2/24(牛源性)、rTexas/37和rMichigan/90(均源性),并在体外和体内比较了它们与野生型牛TX2/24毒株的感染动态、复制和致病性。该研究是在小鼠和仓鼠模型中进行的。
核心方法
研究人员利用反向遗传学拯救了这些病毒,并在体外和体内比较了它们的感染动态、复制和致病性。他们评估了哺乳动物细胞系中的病毒复制,并在小鼠和仓鼠中进行了体内研究,以评估致病性和 传播。
主要结果
所有四种病毒在四种哺乳动物细胞系中都表现出类似的复制动力学。rMichigan/90 菌株在牛和人细胞中表现出较小的斑块。体内研究表明,感染任何一种病毒的小鼠都会在 4-5 天内死亡。然而,与其他毒株相比,感染 rMichigan/90 病毒的小鼠表现出略低的病毒复制和脱落。仓鼠模型显示出比小鼠更温和的感染表型,所有病毒都会导致体重减轻和口腔脱落,但 rMichigan/90 病毒的复制和脱落减少。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,牛源和人源 H5N1 基因型 B3.13 病毒在啮齿动物模型中均表现出高致病性,但在仓鼠模型中总体呼吸道传播率仍然较低。这些结果凸显了 H5N1 病毒在哺乳动物中的高致病性,并证明其呼吸道传播潜力在仓鼠模型中仍然较低。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对H5N1病毒在哺乳动物模型中的致病性和传播潜力的见解,这可以为监测和禽流感 暴发的风险评估策略及其对人类健康的潜在影响提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和体内模型,可能无法完全代表自然的 传播 动力学。证据仅基于抽象数据,并且多个模型的结果是一致的,表明研究结果具有中等可信度。

Infection and transmission dynamics of bovine and human influenza A H5N1 viruses in mouse and hamster models

Authors: Mohammed Nooruzzaman, David W. Buchholz, Ruchi Rani, Pablo Sebastian Britto de Oliveira, Richard A. Adeleke, Chen Feng, Nicholas Vazquez, Elena A. Demeter, Hector C. Aguilar, Diego G. Diel
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Here we investigated the pathogenesis and contact transmission of bovine- and human-derived highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b genotype B3.13 viruses in mammalian models. Using reverse genetics, we rescued three naturally occurring viruses, rTX2/24 (bovine-derived), rTexas/37, and rMichigan/90 (both human-derived), and compared their infection dynamics, replication, and pathogenicity with the wild-type bovine TX2/24 strain in vitro and in vivo . All four viruses demonstrated comparable replication kinetics in four mammalian cell lines. However, the rMichigan/90 strain exhibited smaller plaques in bovine and human cells. In vivo studies showed that mice infected with any of the viruses succumbed to infection within 4–5 days; however, mice infected with the rMichigan/90 virus exhibited slightly lower viral replication and shedding compared with the other strains. The hamster model showed a milder infection phenotype than mice in which all viruses induced body weight loss and oral shedding, with robust virus replication observed in tissues, but the rMichigan/90 virus presented reduced replication and shedding. Contact transmission studies in hamsters revealed limited transmissibility for these viruses, with only one out of four animals inoculated with the rMichigan/90 virus transmitting it to a naïve contact. These findings indicate that both bovine- and human-derived H5N1 genotype B3.13 viruses present high pathogenicity in rodent models, though the overall respiratory transmissibility remains low in the hamster model. IMPORTANCE Influenza A H5N1 virus spilled over from wild birds into dairy cattle in the United States in 2024. Since then, an increased number of human infections—with at least 71 confirmed cases—have been confirmed. In the present study, we evaluated and compared the pathogenicity and transmissibility of a bovine and two human H5N1 viruses of the genotype B3.13. Our results show that while all viruses are pathogenic in mouse and hamster models, the bovine isolate TX2/24 presents a broader tissue tropism than the human-derived viruses. Contact transmission studies revealed a limited ability of these viruses to transmit in the hamster contact transmission model. These findings highlight the high pathogenicity of H5N1 viruses in mammals and demonstrate that their respiratory transmissibility potential remains low in the hamster model.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the pathogenesis and contact transmission of bovine- and human-derived highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b genotype B3.13 viruses in mammalian models. The background highlights the spillover of H5N1 virus from wild birds into dairy cattle in the United States in 2024, leading to an increased number of human infections.
Design and population
The study used reverse genetics to rescue three naturally occurring viruses, rTX2/24 (bovine-derived), rTexas/37, and rMichigan/90 (both human-derived), and compared their infection dynamics, replication, and pathogenicity with the wild-type bovine TX2/24 strain in vitro and in vivo. The study was conducted in mouse and hamster models.
Core methods
The researchers used reverse genetics to rescue the viruses and compared their infection dynamics, replication, and pathogenicity in vitro and in vivo. They evaluated viral replication in mammalian cell lines and performed in vivo studies in mice and hamsters to assess pathogenicity and transmission.
Main results
All four viruses demonstrated comparable replication kinetics in four mammalian cell lines. The rMichigan/90 strain exhibited smaller plaques in bovine and human cells. In vivo studies showed that mice infected with any of the viruses succumbed to infection within 4–5 days; however, mice infected with the rMichigan/90 virus exhibited slightly lower viral replication and shedding compared with the other strains. The hamster model showed a milder infection phenotype than mice, with all viruses inducing body weight loss and oral shedding, but the rMichigan/90 virus presented reduced replication and shedding.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that both bovine- and human-derived H5N1 genotype B3.13 viruses present high pathogenicity in rodent models, though the overall respiratory transmissibility remains low in the hamster model. These results highlight the high pathogenicity of H5N1 viruses in mammals and demonstrate that their respiratory transmissibility potential remains low in the hamster model.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the pathogenicity and transmission potential of H5N1 viruses in mammalian models, which can inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies for avian influenza outbreaks and their potential impact on human health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo models, which may not fully represent natural transmission dynamics. The evidence is based on abstract-only data, and the results are consistent across multiple models, suggesting a moderate confidence level in the findings.

魁北克养鸭场与高致病性 禽流感 相关的生物安全实践和生产特征:2022-2023 年病例对照研究

Biosecurity Practices and Production Characteristics Associated with High-Pathogenicity Avian Influenza in Québec Duck Farms: A 2022–2023 Case-Control Study
作者： Vitória R Lima-Campêlo, Jean‐Pierre Vaillancourt, Jasmine Labrèche, Camille Maheu, Manon Racicot, Vitoria R. Lima Campelo
摘要中文翻译2021 年在加拿大开始的高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) 流行病继续影响家禽种群,导致到 2026 年损失数百万只家禽。为了调查与 HPAI 感染相关的农场层面的风险因素,对加拿大魁北克省的 28 个商业养鸭场进行了病例对照研究。农场完成了一份关于生物安全实践和生产特征的改编 Biocheck.Ugent 调查问卷。使用 Fisher 精确检验和 Wilcoxon 秩和检验评估与 HPAI 状态(病例或对照)的关联,并使用多重对应分析 (MCA) 和主成分分层聚类 (HCPC) 探索多变量模式。与对照农场相比,案例农场的鸭子年产量更高,并且更多地成为综合生产网络的一部分。他们还报告说,对野生动物和结构的检查频率较低,并且更频繁地购买或生产鸭子进行强制喂食。MCA 显示病例农场和对照农场之间有明显的区别,这是由于生物安全实践(例如卫生习惯、停工时间)和农场特征(例如鸡群规模、室内鸡舍类型)的差异所致。HCPC 确定了三个农场集群,其中一个完全由案例农场组成,其特点是强制饲喂生产和畜舍间卫生状况较差。这些发现表明,在识别高致病性禽流感感染风险较高的农场并制定有针对性的预防和控制策略时,应考虑生产结构和生物安全状况。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定与魁北克鸭场高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) 感染相关的农场层面的风险因素,因为自 2021 年以来加拿大的 HPAI 流行已造成家禽数量大幅减少。
设计与对象
2022 年至 2023 年期间,对加拿大魁北克省的 28 个商业养鸭场进行了一项病例对照研究,以调查与 HPAI 感染相关的风险因素。
核心方法
使用 Fisher 精确检验和 Wilcoxon 秩和检验评估与 HPAI 状态的关联,并使用多重对应分析 (MCA) 和主成分分层聚类 (HCPC) 探索多变量模式。
主要结果
与对照农场相比,案例农场的鸭子年产量更高,并且更多地成为综合生产网络的一部分。他们还报告说,对野生动物和结构的检查频率较低,并且更频繁地购买或生产鸭子进行强制喂食。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在识别高致病性禽流感感染风险较高的农场并制定有针对性的预防和控制策略时,应考虑生产结构和生物安全状况。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为监测和鸭场HPAI风险评估策略提供参考,帮助设计更有效的生物安全措施和有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,研究结果基于农场的自我报告数据,这可能会引入偏差。证据强度中等。

Biosecurity Practices and Production Characteristics Associated with High-Pathogenicity Avian Influenza in Québec Duck Farms: A 2022–2023 Case-Control Study

Authors: Vitória R Lima-Campêlo, Jean‐Pierre Vaillancourt, Jasmine Labrèche, Camille Maheu, Manon Racicot, Vitoria R. Lima Campelo
Original abstract (English)
The high-pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) epidemic that began in 2021 in Canada continues to affect poultry populations, resulting in the loss of millions of birds through 2026. To investigate farm-level risk factors associated with HPAI infection, a case–control study was conducted on 28 commercial duck farms in Québec, Canada. Farms completed an adapted Biocheck.Ugent questionnaire on biosecurity practices and production characteristics. Associations with HPAI status (case or control) were assessed using Fisher’s exact and Wilcoxon rank-sum tests, and multivariate patterns were explored using multiple correspondence analysis (MCA) followed by hierarchical clustering on principal components (HCPC). Case farms had higher annual duck production and were more often part of an integrated production network than control farms. They also reported less frequent wildlife and structural inspections and more often purchased or produced ducks for force-feeding. MCA showed clear separation between case and control farms, driven by differences in biosecurity practices (e.g., hygiene practices, downtime) and farm characteristics (e.g., flock size, indoor housing type). HCPC identified three farm clusters, including one composed exclusively of case farms, characterized by force-feeding production and weaker between-barn hygiene. These findings suggest that production structure and biosecurity profiles should be considered when identifying farms at higher risk of HPAI infection and developing targeted prevention and control strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify farm-level risk factors associated with high-pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) infection in Québec duck farms, as the HPAI epidemic in Canada has caused significant poultry population losses since 2021.
Design and population
A case–control study was conducted on 28 commercial duck farms in Québec, Canada, during 2022–2023 to investigate risk factors related to HPAI infection.
Core methods
Associations with HPAI status were assessed using Fisher’s exact and Wilcoxon rank-sum tests, and multivariate patterns were explored using multiple correspondence analysis (MCA) followed by hierarchical clustering on principal components (HCPC).
Main results
Case farms had higher annual duck production and were more often part of an integrated production network than control farms. They also reported less frequent wildlife and structural inspections and more often purchased or produced ducks for force-feeding.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that production structure and biosecurity profiles should be considered when identifying farms at higher risk of HPAI infection and developing targeted prevention and control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies for HPAI in duck farms, helping to design more effective biosecurity measures and targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the findings are based on self-reported data from farms, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate.
