一种病毒聚合酶基因导致温度耐受性复制

Avian influenza A virus polymerase gene leads to temperature-resistant genome replication
作者： Stephanie L. Williams, Sebastian M. Gygli, Jacob C. Bjorgen, Haitian Fan, Munashe Mudiwa, Lex Matthews, Yongli Xiao, Ashley Freeman, Sharon F. Legaspi, Li Qi
摘要中文翻译摘要 流感A病毒(IAV)对人类健康构成持续的流行威胁。从水禽到哺乳动物的成功跨物种传播需要IAV进行适应性改变以实现高效的复制和传播。禽类IAV适应于禽类肠道的温度,约为41°C,而人类IAV则在上呼吸道的33°C温度下复制。PB1是病毒异三聚体RNA依赖性RNA聚合酶(RdRp)的催化核心,对于病毒RNA合成至关重要。三种大流行IAV株(1918年、1957年和1968年)携带了源自禽类的PB1基因,这表明其具有选择优势。发热是一种关键的免疫反应,会将体温升高至38°C或更高,并可能限制病毒复制。我们假设禽类PB1可能允许IAV在发热温度下复制。利用一种微型复制子系统,我们证明了季节性人类H1N1 IAV的聚合酶在40°C下无法高效复制病毒RNA,而将PB1替换为源自1918年、1957年和1968年大流行病毒或低致病性禽类H2N1病毒的PB1基因后,RNA合成得以恢复。在MDCK细胞中,表达禽类或1918年PB1的季节性人类H1N1病毒株可以在40°C下复制,而野生型H1N1病毒则不能。使用纯化的嵌合IAV聚合酶进行活性测定进一步表明,源自禽类的PB1可能在高温下内在性地增强聚合酶活性。这些数据表明,源自禽类的PB1基因使IAV能够在发热温度下复制,突显了其在人兽共患病出现和适应性改变中的作用。在哺乳动物中流行的IAV中,源自禽类的PB1基因的特征可能有助于识别具有大流行潜力的病毒株,并为开发应对措施提供信息。重要性 源自禽类的流感A病毒仍然是人类健康的一个持续威胁。1918年、1957年和1968年的大流行流感病毒都获得了源自禽类的PB1基因,但这些基因所带来的适应性优势仍不完全清楚。在这里,我们展示了源自禽类的PB1基因如何增强季节性人类流感病毒聚合酶在高温下的病毒RNA复制能力,并使人类流感病毒能够在40°C下复制,这一温度与禽类和人类发热疾病均相关。在基于细胞的聚合酶实验中,以及在纯化的聚合酶和真实病毒感染过程中均观察到了PB1依赖性效应。这些发现将温度耐受性复制识别为源自禽类PB1的潜在适应性特征,并表明获得禽类PB1可能有助于流感病毒在适应哺乳动物宿主时克服温度障碍。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感A病毒(IAV)对人类健康构成持续的流行威胁。从水禽到哺乳动物的成功跨物种传播需要IAV进行适应性改变以实现高效的复制和传播。
设计与对象
该研究调查了源自鸟类的 PB1 基因在哺乳动物 宿主 的 IAV 中的复制情况。三种大流行IAV毒株(1918年、1957年和1968年)携带禽源PB1基因,表明具有选择优势。
核心方法
研究人员使用微型复制子系统证明,季节性人类 H1N1 IAV 的聚合酶在 40°C 下无法有效复制病毒 RNA,而用 1918 年、1957 年和 1968 年大流行病毒或低致病性禽 H2N1 病毒的 PB1 替换,可以恢复 RNA 合成。
主要结果
在MDCK细胞中,表达禽类或1918年PB1的季节性人类H1N1病毒株可以在40°C下复制,而野生型H1N1病毒则不能。纯化嵌合IAV聚合酶的活性测定进一步表明,源自禽类的PB1可能在高温下内在性地增强聚合酶活性。
解释与创新
这些发现将耐温 基因组 复制确定为禽源 PB1 的潜在适应特性,并表明获得禽类 PB1 可能有助于流感病毒克服在适应哺乳动物 宿主 过程中遇到的温度障碍。
科研与公卫意义
在哺乳动物中流行的IAV中,源自禽类的PB1基因的特征可能有助于识别具有大流行潜力的病毒株,并为开发应对措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或方法的具体限制。

Avian influenza A virus polymerase gene leads to temperature-resistant genome replication

Authors: Stephanie L. Williams, Sebastian M. Gygli, Jacob C. Bjorgen, Haitian Fan, Munashe Mudiwa, Lex Matthews, Yongli Xiao, Ashley Freeman, Sharon F. Legaspi, Li Qi
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Influenza A viruses (IAV) pose a persistent pandemic threat. Successful cross-species transmission from waterfowl to mammals requires host adaptation for efficient replication and spread. Avian IAVs are adapted to the temperature of the avian gut, approximately 41°C, while human IAVs replicate at 33°C in the upper respiratory tract. PB1, the catalytic core of the viral heterotrimeric RNA-dependent RNA polymerase (RdRp), is essential for viral RNA synthesis. Three pandemic IAV strains (1918, 1957, and 1968) carried an avian-origin PB1 gene, suggesting a selective advantage. Fever, a critical antiviral response, elevates body temperature to 38°C or higher and can restrict viral replication. We hypothesized that avian PB1 may allow IAV replication at febrile temperatures. Using a mini-replicon system, we demonstrate that a polymerase of a seasonal human H1N1 IAV fails to replicate viral RNA efficiently at 40°C, whereas substitution of the PB1 with that from the 1918, 1957, and 1968 pandemic viruses, or a low-pathogenic avian H2N1 virus, restores RNA synthesis. Chimeric seasonal human H1N1 viruses expressing avian or 1918 PB1 replicate at 40°C in MDCK cells, unlike wild-type H1N1. Activity assays with purified chimeric IAV polymerases further suggest that avian-origin PB1 may intrinsically enhance polymerase activity at elevated temperatures. Together, these data show that avian-origin PB1 enables IAV replication at febrile temperatures, highlighting its role in zoonotic emergence and host adaptation. Surveillance of avian-origin PB1 genes in IAVs circulating in mammals may help identify strains with pandemic potential and inform the development of countermeasures. IMPORTANCE Influenza A viruses of avian origin remain a persistent threat to human health. The 1918, 1957, and 1968 pandemic influenza viruses each acquired an avian-origin PB1 gene, but the adaptive advantage conferred by these genes remains incompletely understood. Here, we show that avian-origin PB1 genes enhance viral RNA replication by a seasonal human influenza virus polymerase at elevated temperature and enable replication of a human influenza virus at 40°C, a temperature relevant to both avian hosts and human febrile illness. PB1-dependent effects were observed in cell-based polymerase assays, with purified polymerase, and during authentic virus infection. These findings identify temperature-resistant genome replication as a potential adaptive property of avian-origin PB1 and suggest that acquisition of an avian PB1 may help influenza viruses overcome temperature barriers encountered during adaptation to mammalian hosts.

Design and population

Core methods

Main results
Chimeric seasonal human H1N1 viruses expressing avian or 1918 PB1 replicate at 40°C in MDCK cells, unlike wild-type H1N1. Activity assays with purified chimeric IAV polymerases further suggest that avian-origin PB1 may intrinsically enhance polymerase activity at elevated temperatures.
Interpretation and novelty

为什么一种健康会带来回报:来自喀麦隆 2016 年 H5N1 暴发 的经济证据

Why One Health Pays Off: Economic Evidence from Cameroon’s 2016 H5N1 Outbreak
作者： Elisabeth B. Dibongue, Amadou Nchare, Jean-Marc Feusson, Cha A. Crystella, Ikoum Dalida, Conrad N. Nkuo, Ambroise Garga, Sali Ballo, Salla Abdou, Gaston Djonwe
摘要中文翻译摘要 喀麦隆在 2016 年经历了一次 H5N1 禽流感大流行,影响了多个地区的家禽养殖场、市场和生产场所。由于该国此前未对其 暴发 应对措施进行经济评估,因此进行了成本分析,以记录 暴发 应对措施的财务和运营要求,并为支持准备计划和未来经济评估提供证据。该研究旨在记录与 暴发 响应相关的财务和运营成本,并确定可能为未来经济评估和准备规划提供信息的关键数据和报告差距。成本分析使用了实验室测试、净化活动、受感染家禽和材料的销毁以及 暴发 响应期间进行的现场操作的可用数据。信息由国家实验室、兽医当局、公共卫生参与者和官方报告提供。然而,不完整的文件记录和未分类的财务记录需要在成本重建期间做出一些假设。该分析记录了实施应对措施的成本,包括实验室检测、净化、销毁受感染的家禽和材料,以及 暴发 应对期间进行的现场操作。调查结果表明,应对措施涉及大量运营和财务成本,但结果的准确性受到可用数据有限、缺乏详细财务报告以及运营信息差距的限制。评估还强调了重要的操作要素,包括多实验室样本处理和现场净化所需的大量工作。它还提请人们注意来源分析中考虑的更广泛的家禽业影响,包括生产连续性和与肉、蛋和粪便相关的估计价值。该研究深入了解了多部门 暴发 应对措施的运营和财务要求,同时强调了加强“同一个健康”应对措施的准备、记录和未来经济评估的重要考虑因素。信息 © 作者 2026
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在记录与 暴发 响应相关的财务和运营成本,并确定可能为未来经济评估和准备规划提供信息的关键数据和报告差距。
设计与对象
该研究是在 2016 年 H5N1 暴发 期间在喀麦隆进行的,分析了多个地区的家禽养殖场、市场和生产场所的成本。
核心方法
成本分析使用了实验室测试、净化活动、受感染家禽和材料的销毁以及 暴发 响应期间进行的现场操作的可用数据。
主要结果
该分析记录了实施应对措施的成本,包括实验室检测、净化、销毁受感染的家禽和材料,以及 暴发 应对期间进行的现场操作。
解释与创新
该研究深入了解了多部门 暴发 应对措施的运营和财务要求,同时强调了加强“同一个健康”应对措施的准备、记录和未来经济评估的重要考虑因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为准备计划、经济评估以及为未来的 暴发 应对措施开发更强大的文件和报告系统提供信息。
局限与证据强度
不完整的文件记录和未分类的财务记录在成本重建过程中需要进行多种假设,这可能会限制结果的准确性。

Why One Health Pays Off: Economic Evidence from Cameroon’s 2016 H5N1 Outbreak

Authors: Elisabeth B. Dibongue, Amadou Nchare, Jean-Marc Feusson, Cha A. Crystella, Ikoum Dalida, Conrad N. Nkuo, Ambroise Garga, Sali Ballo, Salla Abdou, Gaston Djonwe
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Cameroon experienced a major H5N1 epizootic in 2016, affecting poultry farms, markets, and production sites across several regions. Because the country had not previously undertaken an economic evaluation of its outbreak response, a cost analysis was conducted to document the financial and operational requirements of outbreak response and provide evidence to support preparedness planning and future economic evaluations. The study aimed to document the financial and operational costs associated with the outbreak response and identify key data and reporting gaps that may inform future economic evaluations and preparedness planning. The cost analysis used available data from laboratory testing, decontamination activities, destruction of infected poultry and materials, and field operations undertaken during the outbreak response. Information was provided by national laboratories, veterinary authorities, public health actors, and official reports. However, incomplete documentation and non-disaggregated financial records required several assumptions during cost reconstruction. The analysis documented the costs of implementing response measures, including laboratory testing, decontamination, destruction of infected poultry and materials, and field operations undertaken during the outbreak response. Findings indicate that the response involved substantial operational and financial costs, but the accuracy of results was constrained by limited data availability, lack of detailed financial reporting, and gaps in operational information. The assessment also highlighted important operational elements, including multi-laboratory sample handling and the extensive effort required for site decontamination. It also draws attention to broader poultry-sector effects considered in the source analysis, including production continuity and estimated value linked to meat, eggs, and manure. The study provides insight into the operational and financial requirements of multisectoral outbreak response, while highlighting important considerations for strengthening preparedness, documentation, and future economic assessment of One Health responses. Information © The Authors 2026

The study aimed to document the financial and operational costs associated with the outbreak response and identify key data and reporting gaps that may inform future economic evaluations and preparedness planning.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Cameroon during the 2016 H5N1 outbreak, analyzing costs across poultry farms, markets, and production sites in several regions.
Core methods
The cost analysis used available data from laboratory testing, decontamination activities, destruction of infected poultry and materials, and field operations undertaken during the outbreak response.
Main results
The analysis documented the costs of implementing response measures, including laboratory testing, decontamination, destruction of infected poultry and materials, and field operations undertaken during the outbreak response.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides insight into the operational and financial requirements of multisectoral outbreak response, while highlighting important considerations for strengthening preparedness, documentation, and future economic assessment of One Health responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform preparedness planning, economic evaluations, and the development of more robust documentation and reporting systems for future outbreak responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
Incomplete documentation and non-disaggregated financial records required several assumptions during cost reconstruction, which may limit the accuracy of the results.

线性DNA 疫苗:克服经典DNA 疫苗局限性的新方法,以禽流感 A/H5预防为例

Linear DNA vaccine: a new approach to overcome the limitations of classical DNA vaccines, using avian influenza A/H5 prevention as an example
作者： Mariya Borisovna Borgoyakova, N. Rudometova, V. А. Filatova, Дарья Александровна Филиппова, Ekaterina V. Starostina, E. V. Yakovleva, Andrei S. Gudymo, O. N. Perfileva, Ksenia I. Ivanova, Kristina P. Makarova
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses of the A/H5 subtype pose a serious threat to public health due to their high mortality rate (over 50 % among reported cases) and the potential risk of acquiring the ability to transmit between humans.
设计与对象
开发编码 A/H5 亚型 高致病性禽流感 病毒血凝素的线性 DNA 疫苗,并评估其在 BALB/c 小鼠中的免疫原性和保护功效。
核心方法
线性 DNA 疫苗 db-H5 是使用 DoggyboneTM 技术以及 φ29 DNA 聚合酶和 TelN 原核端粒酶生产的。通过蛋白质印迹分析评估 db-H5 的体外功能。使用 ELISA、病毒中和和 IFN-γ ELISpot 测定评估免疫原性。
主要结果
Mice immunized with the db‐H5 DNA vaccine showed high titres of haemagglutinin‐specific antibodies (1:72 900), neutralizing antibodies (median titre 1:40), and increased numbers of IFN‐γ‐producing splenocytes (48 SFUs/106). The survival rate of immunized mice after lethal challenge with 20MLD50 was 70 %, whereas all animals in the control group died.
解释与创新
结果表明,线性DNA 疫苗 db-H5能够有效诱导小鼠针对高致病性禽流感 A/H5病毒的保护性免疫,这是DoggyboneTM技术在俄罗斯实践中的新应用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,线性 DNA 疫苗 平台可能是开发快速有效的 疫苗 对抗 高致病性禽流感 A/H5 病毒的宝贵工具,这对公共卫生具有重大影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠体内测试,结果可能无法直接转化为人类应用。然而,鉴于在动物模型中清楚地证明了免疫原性和保护功效,证据是强有力的。

Linear DNA vaccine: a new approach to overcome the limitations of classical DNA vaccines, using avian influenza A/H5 prevention as an example

Authors: Mariya Borisovna Borgoyakova, N. Rudometova, V. А. Filatova, Дарья Александровна Филиппова, Ekaterina V. Starostina, E. V. Yakovleva, Andrei S. Gudymo, O. N. Perfileva, Ksenia I. Ivanova, Kristina P. Makarova
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses of the A/H5 subtype pose a serious threat to public health due to their high mortality rate (over 50 % among reported cases) and the potential risk of acquiring the ability to transmit between humans. Developing effective vaccine platforms, including DNA vaccines, is a priority task. The DoggyboneTM linear DNA vaccine technology overcomes the limitations of classical plasmid DNA vaccines (the presence of bacterial elements and antibiotic resistance genes) while retaining their advantages, such as high production speed that does not require using cell cultures or chicken embryos, temperature stability, and cost‐effectiveness. Developing a linear DNA vaccine encoding the haemagglutinin of the highly pathogenic avian influenza virus of the A/H5 subtype and evaluation its immunogenicity and protective efficacy in BALB/c mice. Materials and methods. The linear DNA vaccine db‐H5 was produced using the DoggyboneTM technology with φ29 DNA polymerase and TelN protelomerase. The functionality of db‐H5 in vitro was assessed by Western blot analysis. For the in vivo study, BALB/c mice were immunized with 1 μg of the DNA vaccine via jet injection. Immunogenicity was evaluated using ELISA, virus neutralization, and IFN‐γ ELISpot assays. Protective efficacy was tested by challenging the animals with the A/Astrakhan/3212/2020 (H5N8) virus. The resulting linear DNA ensured the expression of the target gene in transfected HEK293 cells. Mice immunized with the db‐H5 DNA vaccine showed high titres of haemagglutinin‐specific antibodies (1:72 900), neutralizing antibodies (median titre 1:40), and increased numbers of IFN‐γ‐producing splenocytes (48 SFUs/106). The survival rate of immunized mice after lethal challenge with 20MLD50 was 70 %, whereas all animals in the control group died. For the first time in Russian practice, we have demonstrated the feasibility of developing a linear DNA vaccine against diseases caused by highly pathogenic avian influenza A/H5 viruses using the DoggyboneTM technology. Two‐dose immunization of mice with an low dose (1 μg) provides protection against lethal infection, which justifies further development of this platform.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses of the A/H5 subtype pose a serious threat to public health due to their high mortality rate (over 50 % among reported cases) and the potential risk of acquiring the ability to transmit between humans.
Design and population
Developing a linear DNA vaccine encoding the haemagglutinin of the highly pathogenic avian influenza virus of the A/H5 subtype and evaluation its immunogenicity and protective efficacy in BALB/c mice.
Core methods
The linear DNA vaccine db‐H5 was produced using the DoggyboneTM technology with φ29 DNA polymerase and TelN protelomerase. The functionality of db‐H5 in vitro was assessed by Western blot analysis. Immunogenicity was evaluated using ELISA, virus neutralization, and IFN‐γ ELISpot assays.
Main results
Mice immunized with the db‐H5 DNA vaccine showed high titres of haemagglutinin‐specific antibodies (1:72 900), neutralizing antibodies (median titre 1:40), and increased numbers of IFN‐γ‐producing splenocytes (48 SFUs/106). The survival rate of immunized mice after lethal challenge with 20MLD50 was 70 %, whereas all animals in the control group died.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate that the linear DNA vaccine db‐H5 is effective in inducing protective immunity against highly pathogenic avian influenza A/H5 viruses in mice, which is a novel application of the DoggyboneTM technology in Russian practice.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the linear DNA vaccine platform could be a valuable tool for developing rapid and effective vaccines against highly pathogenic avian influenza A/H5 viruses, which have significant public health implications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vivo testing in mice, and the results may not directly translate to human applications. However, the evidence is strong given the clear demonstration of immunogenicity and protective efficacy in the animal model.

《哨兵》是对禽流感和疯狂资本主义采取行动的紧急呼吁。读书也是一种享受

Sentinels is an urgent call to action on bird flu, and capitalism gone wild. It’s also a joy to read
作者： Euan Ritchie
摘要中文翻译书评:《哨兵》迈克尔·杜兰尼(Scribe Publications)著。截至目前,澳大利亚已发生超过700起禽流感事件——这些事件包括单个或多个动物死亡。这是对我们未能保护自然、防止病原体在非人类动物与人类之间传播的警示。‘哨兵’这一概念源自疾病研究,正如流行病学,因此这本书无疑具有全球相关性。杜兰尼写道:‘有时,当安格加发现一种极为罕见的青蛙或蛇类物种时,他的客户会因为找到他们一生都在寻找的东西而泪流满面。’杜兰尼认为,高致病性禽流感(H5N1禽流感)是当今时代最具代表性的疾病,它体现了当疾病在动物与人类之间传播时所发生的情况。这种疾病现已影响澳大利亚大陆的野生动物,它于6月传入澳大利亚,象征着我们对自然的冷漠忽视,尽管我们深深嵌入并依赖于自然。‘在我所了解的所有病原体中,这无疑是最大的一个,’杜兰尼写道。更大的冠tern、小企鹅、普通海豚、长吻海豹、澳大利亚海狮以及许多其他鸟类和哺乳动物物种都已受到影响。在本书中,杜兰尼深入探讨了我们对非人类生命的影响以及与它们的联系。我们仍在以惊人的规模和速度开发物种并掠夺自然。据估计,人类开发的物种数量是非人类捕食者的300倍。杜兰尼的论点是,这些哨兵正变得越来越虚弱,常常在...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究强调了 禽流感 A 病毒(尤其是 H5N1 禽流感)作为我们这个时代的一种决定性疾病的日益严重的威胁,并强调需要解决动物和人类之间不断增加的 跨物种溢出 病原体问题。
设计与对象
该研究是对 Michael Dulaney 所著《哨兵》一书的评论,该书研究了 禽流感 A 病毒(包括 H5N1)在澳大利亚和全球不同生态系统和物种中的传播,重点关注野生动物、农业和人类健康影响。
核心方法
这些方法包括对本书进行叙述性回顾,分析禽流感 A病毒暴发(包括H5N1)在不同地区和物种中的案例研究和实例,并讨论导致疾病跨物种溢出的生态和社会因素。
主要结果
该书报道了澳大利亚发生的 700 多起禽流感事件,估计有超过 13,000 只象海豹幼崽在赫德岛死亡,并强调了人畜共患流感 暴发 的发生频率不断增加,在过去三十年中约有 200 种新的传染病传染给人类。
解释与创新
杜兰尼将这些发现解释为人类活动破坏栖息地并使野生动物和家畜更接近人类的可预测结果,这表明 禽流感 A 病毒,特别是 H5N1,代表了更广泛的 跨物种溢出 疾病和环境开发危机。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要更好的 监测 和保护生态系统,以降低人畜共患流感 暴发 的风险,强调环境健康和公共卫生的相互关联性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于书评,不包括原始数据或统计分析,这限制了其证据强度。然而,它全面概述了 禽流感 A 病毒传播的生态和社会背景。

Sentinels is an urgent call to action on bird flu, and capitalism gone wild. It’s also a joy to read

Authors: Euan Ritchie
Original abstract (English)
Review: Sentinels by Michael Dulaney (Scribe Publications) Over 700 bird flu events – which cover individual or multiple deaths of animals – have occurred in Australia so far. It’s a sobering result of our missed chances to protect nature and prevent pathogens from jumping between non-human animals and people. The concept of a sentinel — the canary in the coal mine — draws from epidemiology, the study of disease. As a result, this book is undoubtedly globally relevant. “Sometimes, when Angga uncovers an exceptionally rare frog or snake species, his clients break down crying over finding something they have searched for their whole lives,” he writes. Highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1 bird flu) is the defining disease of our times, suggests Australian writer and journalist Michael Dulaney in his timely book Sentinels, on what happens when diseases spread between animals and humans. Now impacting mainland Australian wildlife, the disease, which reached us in June, epitomises our callous neglect of nature, despite us being embedded in and reliant on it. “Of all the pathogens I’ve learned about, this is the big one,” Dulaney writes. Greater crested terns, little penguins, common bottlenose dolphin, long-nosed fur seals, Australian sea lions, and many other bird and mammal species have all succumbed. In his book, which was longlisted for this year’s Walkley Book Award and published just a few months after bird flu reached Australia, Dulaney thoughtfully discusses our impacts on and connections with non-human life. We continue to exploit species and plunder nature at an alarming scale and rate. It’s estimated humans exploit as much as 300 times more species than non-human predators. Dulaney’s thesis is that these sentinels are increasingly sickly, often dropping dead at...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study highlights the growing threat of avian influenza A viruses, particularly H5N1 bird flu, as a defining disease of our times, emphasizing the need to address the increasing spillover of pathogens between animals and humans.
Design and population
The study is a review of the book 'Sentinels' by Michael Dulaney, which examines the spread of avian influenza A viruses, including H5N1, across various ecosystems and species in Australia and globally, focusing on wildlife, agriculture, and human health impacts.
Core methods
The methods involve a narrative review of the book, analyzing case studies and examples of avian influenza A virus outbreaks, including H5N1, in different regions and species, and discussing the ecological and societal factors contributing to disease spillover.
Main results
The book reports over 700 bird flu events in Australia, with more than 13,000 elephant seal pups estimated to have died on Heard Island, and highlights the increasing frequency of zoonotic influenza outbreaks, with around 200 new infectious diseases jumping to humans over the past three decades.
Interpretation and novelty
Dulaney interprets these findings as a predictable outcome of human activities that destroy habitats and bring wildlife and domestic animals closer to humans, suggesting that avian influenza A viruses, particularly H5N1, represent a broader crisis of spillover diseases and environmental exploitation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for better surveillance and protection of ecosystems to reduce the risk of zoonotic influenza outbreaks, emphasizing the interconnectedness of environmental health and public health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a book review and does not include original data or statistical analysis, which limits its evidence strength. However, it provides a comprehensive overview of the ecological and societal context of avian influenza A virus spread.

甲型流感 (H5N1) 和人类:流行病和 Saúde 系统的准备工作

Influenza Aviária A(H5N1) em Mamíferos e Humanos: Risco Pandêmico e Preparação dos Sistemas de Saúde
作者： Marilia Bedani de Oliveira, Piero Biteli
摘要中文翻译甲型H5N1流感禽流感将成为2020年世界性的一个重大事件,对家禽和家禽造成巨大的死亡威胁,并登记了多种感染者的感染情况,包括感染者和海员南美洲,2024 年的一部分,是联合国国家的目标。据介绍,有 70 多个人的孔洞被确认为国家的孔洞,这些孔洞都是感染动物的,一般都是四级和结膜。没有证据表明可持续发展的重要性,并与世界组织、国家统一组织、食品和农业组织、世界动物组织、动物保护组织等相结合。总体而言,在职业展示方面,一切都是一个中庸之道。巴西将在 2025 年开始大规模商业化,并会快速回复内容。整个系统的准备工作包括人类综合警戒、动物和环境、工作人员的保护、诊断和抗病毒药物预防怀疑、疫苗生产计划和风险交流。本文修订了最近的流行病学,作为人类临床的宣言和警戒和准备策略,以巴西的情况和萨乌德乌尼卡的情况为背景。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感(H5N1)是一种导致人类死亡和传染给人类的致命疾病,包括人类、传染病和流行病的传播潜力。
设计与对象
我们对近期禽流感 A(H5N1) 的流行病学进行了研究,重点关注全球传播、感染者和人类确诊情况,特别是在巴西和美国。
核心方法
对文学和官方关系的修订进行了分析和基础分析,包括流行病学、确认案例、警戒策略和安全系统准备。
主要结果
论坛注册了 70 多个国家的人类确认信息,作为感染动物的工作人员的待办事项,并提供四项临床检查和结膜治疗。巴西将在 2025 年开始大规模商业化,并会快速回复内容。
解释与创新
作为国际组织的联合体,考虑到普通民众的风险,也考虑到职业和现代的风险。一项关于保护乌尼卡的计划和与警戒和准备相关的战略。
科研与公卫意义
需要注意的是,必须对人、动物和环境进行综合警戒,保护工作人员的后遗症,并制定疫苗生产计划,以减轻 A(H5N1) 禽流感病毒的危害。
局限与证据强度
对官方信息和相关关系的基础进行分析,以限制结论的深刻性。一个证据并考虑到研究的自然描述。

Influenza Aviária A(H5N1) em Mamíferos e Humanos: Risco Pandêmico e Preparação dos Sistemas de Saúde

Authors: Marilia Bedani de Oliveira, Piero Biteli
Original abstract (English)
A influenza aviária altamente patogênica A(H5N1) espalhou-se pelo mundo a partir de 2020 e provocou grande mortalidade de aves silvestres e domésticas, com registro de infecção em diversas espécies de mamíferos, incluindo mamíferos marinhos na América do Sul e, a partir de 2024, bovinos leiteiros nos Estados Unidos. Desde então, mais de 70 casos humanos foram confirmados naquele país, quase todos em trabalhadores expostos a animais infectados, em geral com quadro leve e conjuntivite. Não há evidência de transmissão sustentada entre pessoas, e as avaliações conjuntas da Organização Mundial da Saúde, da Organização das Nações Unidas para a Alimentação e a Agricultura e da Organização Mundial de Saúde Animal consideram baixo o risco para a população geral e de baixo a moderado para pessoas com exposição ocupacional. No Brasil, o primeiro foco em granja comercial foi confirmado em 2025, com resposta rápida de contenção. A preparação dos sistemas de saúde envolve vigilância integrada humana, animal e ambiental, proteção dos trabalhadores expostos, diagnóstico e tratamento antiviral precoce dos casos suspeitos, planos de produção de vacinas e comunicação de risco. Este capítulo revisa a epidemiologia recente, as manifestações clínicas em humanos e as estratégias de vigilância e preparação, com atenção ao contexto brasileiro e à abordagem de Saúde Única.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A influenza aviária altamente patogênica A(H5N1) tem causado mortalidade significativa em aves e infecções em mamíferos, incluindo humanos, levantando preocupações sobre seu potencial de transmissão e risco pandêmico.
Design and population
O estudo revisa a epidemiologia recente da influenza aviária A(H5N1), com foco em sua disseminação global, infecção em mamíferos e casos humanos confirmados, especialmente nos Estados Unidos e no Brasil.
Core methods
A análise é baseada em revisão da literatura e relatos oficiais, incluindo dados epidemiológicos, casos confirmados e estratégias de vigilância e preparação dos sistemas de saúde.
Main results
Foram registrados mais de 70 casos humanos confirmados nos Estados Unidos, quase todos em trabalhadores expostos a animais infectados, com quadro clínico geralmente leve e conjuntivite. No Brasil, o primeiro foco em granja comercial foi confirmado em 2025, com resposta rápida de contenção.
Interpretation and novelty
As avaliações conjuntas de organizações internacionais consideram o risco para a população geral como baixo, mas o risco para pessoas com exposição ocupacional é moderado. A abordagem de Saúde Única é destacada como uma estratégia relevante para a vigilância e preparação.
Scientific and public-health significance
Os achados informam a necessidade de vigilância integrada entre humanos, animais e o ambiente, proteção dos trabalhadores expostos e desenvolvimento de planos de produção de vacinas para mitigar riscos associados à influenza aviária A(H5N1).
Limitations and evidence strength
A análise é baseada em dados disponíveis e relatos oficiais, o que pode limitar a profundidade das conclusões. A evidência é considerada moderada devido à natureza descritiva do estudo.

蒙古人工巢穴中未检测到 禽流感 A 型病毒

No Detection of Avian Influenza Virus Type A in Broods of Nestling Saker Falcons Falco cherrug at Artificial Nests in Mongolia
作者： Andrew Dixon, Mohamed Saleh Al Baidani, Batbayar Bold, Amarkhuu Gungaa, Mungunbagana Sukh-Ochir, Jugdernamjil Nergui, Makhgal Ganbold, Gankhuyag Purev-Ochir, Unursaikhan Ulaankhuu, Munir Z. Virani
摘要中文翻译最新研究表明,禽流感病毒对小鸟和畜栏的破坏者有很大影响,有利于小鸟迁徙并在世界范围内传播。在蒙古,南亚地区的原始迁徙主要是在圣殿地区的七处繁殖区和可能通过重要的分散途径构成的。在国家中,对禽流感病毒 (VIA) 引起的感染的警戒是对鸟类的主要负责。对 Falco cherrug 的感染情况进行评估,特别是在 CITES 的商业监管中捕获和出口,希望通过传播方式进行感染,特别是国家的保护戈尔福。在蒙古中心和东部的人工安装中,muestreamos 200 nidadas de Falco cherrug midte hisopados orofaríngeos 和 muestras de muda de plamas。没有检测到通过 VIA 检测到的感染情况 A 中级反应在 polimerasa 的 cadena de la polimerasa contranscriptasa invsera 中,显示感染的情况低于 6% de nuestro estudio 的检测范围。[社论翻译]
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估蒙古人工巢中的雏隼 Falco cherrug 中 禽流感 A 病毒的感染率,因为这些鸟类有可能成为 媒介 疾病的 媒介,特别是对海湾地区国家。最近的 高致病性禽流感 对野生和家养鸟类产生了毁灭性影响,促进了病毒通过候鸟在全球传播。
设计与对象
这项研究在蒙古进行,重点关注蒙古中部和东部人工巢中的 200 只雏猎隼。通过口咽拭子和羽毛浆样品收集样品。
核心方法
使用逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 对样本进行 禽流感 A 病毒检测。
主要结果
在采样的 200 只雏隼中均未检测到 禽流感 A 病毒,表明感染率低于研究的 6% 检测限。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明,采集的隼隼并没有充当 禽流感 A 病毒 传播 的重要 媒介,有助于了解候鸟在疾病传播中的作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,隼隼可能不是 禽流感 A 病毒 传播 的重要 媒介,这可以为 监测 和这些鸟类及其栖息地的保护工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括样本量小和使用人工巢,可能无法完全代表猎鹰的自然筑巢行为。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

No Detection of Avian Influenza Virus Type A in Broods of Nestling Saker Falcons Falco cherrug at Artificial Nests in Mongolia

Authors: Andrew Dixon, Mohamed Saleh Al Baidani, Batbayar Bold, Amarkhuu Gungaa, Mungunbagana Sukh-Ochir, Jugdernamjil Nergui, Makhgal Ganbold, Gankhuyag Purev-Ochir, Unursaikhan Ulaankhuu, Munir Z. Virani
Original abstract (English)
Los recientes brotes del altamente patógeno virus de la influenza aviar han tenido efectos devastadores sobre las aves silvestres y de corral, habiendo facilitado las aves migratorias su propagación a escala mundial. En Mongolia, la migración primaveral de aves desde el sudeste asiático hacia las zonas de reproducción más septentrionales de regiones templadas y árticas probablemente constituye una vía importante de dispersión. En este país, la vigilancia de las infecciones provocadas por el virus de la influenza aviar (VIA) se ha centrado principalmente en las aves acuáticas. Aquí evaluamos las tasas de infección por el VIA de tipo A en nidadas de Falco cherrug, una especie que se captura y exporta anualmente en el marco del comercio regulado por la CITES, lo que supone una posible vía de transmisión de enfermedades, en particular hacia los países del Golfo. En nidos artificiales instalados en el centro y este de Mongolia, muestreamos 200 nidadas de Falco cherrug mediante hisopados orofaríngeos y muestras de pulpa de plumas. No detectamos ningún caso de infección por el VIA de tipo A mediante pruebas de reacción en cadena de la polimerasa con transcriptasa invsera, lo que indica que la tasa de infección es inferior al límite de detección del 6% de nuestro estudio. [Traducción del equipo editorial]
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the infection rates of avian influenza A viruses in nestling saker falcons Falco cherrug at artificial nests in Mongolia, given the potential for these birds to act as vectors for disease transmission, particularly to countries in the Gulf region. Recent outbreaks of highly pathogenic avian influenza have had devastating effects on wild and domestic birds, facilitating the global spread of the virus through migratory birds.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Mongolia, focusing on 200 nestling saker falcons Falco cherrug at artificial nests in central and eastern Mongolia. Samples were collected through oropharyngeal swabs and feather pulp samples.
Core methods
Samples were tested for avian influenza A viruses using reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR).
Main results
No avian influenza A virus was detected in any of the 200 nestling saker falcons sampled, indicating an infection rate below the 6% detection limit of the study.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that the saker falcons sampled did not serve as a significant vector for avian influenza A virus transmission, contributing to the understanding of the role of migratory birds in disease spread.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that saker falcons may not be a significant vector for avian influenza A virus transmission, which could inform surveillance and conservation efforts for these birds and their habitats.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the small sample size and the use of artificial nests, which may not fully represent the natural nesting behavior of saker falcons. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

鸡干扰素刺激的基因筛选揭示了限制流感病毒复制的有限基因

A chicken interferon-stimulated gene screen reveals limited genes restricting influenza virus replication
作者： Alison J. Barkhymer, Jordan T. Becker, Frances K. Shepherd, Clayton K. Mickelson, Lauren M. Pross, Mark Salnikov, Ryan Andrew Langlois
摘要中文翻译摘要 禽流感 病毒 (IAV) 是全球主要的健康威胁。它不仅给农业造成重大经济损失,而且还给人类带来了跨物种溢出的风险。然而,要成功开展跨物种 传播 活动,必须克服一些障碍。这些障碍之一是 宿主 的先天免疫。虽然人类对 IAV 的先天 免疫应答 已进行了充分研究,但鸟类对 IAV 的先天 免疫应答 尚不清楚。在这里,我们通过鉴定鸡、鹌鹑和鸭细胞中的干扰素刺激基因(ISG)来探索干扰素诱导的 免疫应答,并发现它们很大程度上具有物种特异性。利用这些数据,我们对两种 IAV 株(小鼠适应性 PR8 和低致病性禽 IAV 分离株 WF10)进行了 CRISPR 敲除 ISG 筛选。我们发现,对于 PR8,IFITM3 是病毒复制的主要限制因子,还有一些额外的作用,但效果适中,这与之前在哺乳动物中的研究结果一致。对于禽类 WF10,IFITM3 也很受欢迎;然而,PR8 还缺少另外两个命中:HSP70 辅助伴侣蛋白 BAG5 和去泛素酶 OTUD4。总体而言,这些发现证实了物种之间干扰素介导的免疫的差异,并建立了一个筛选系统,可用于识别对禽类病毒限制重要的 ISG。重要性 储存宿主,特别是水禽和滨鸟,是甲型流感病毒的主要天然 储存宿主。这些鸟类可以将 IAV 传播给家禽,从而增加人类的 跨物种溢出 风险。鸡是全世界最常见的家禽品种,经常感染 禽流感 病毒。尽管如此,人们对它们的免疫系统如何应对流感感染知之甚少。这种知识的缺乏为寻找预防或减轻鸡群 IAV 感染的方法造成了障碍。在这项研究中,我们对干扰素诱导的 免疫应答 对鸡 IAV 进行了表征,发现只有一小部分 ISG 负责限制鸡细胞中的 IAV。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 病毒 (IAV) 是一种主要的全球健康威胁,不仅给农业造成重大经济损失,而且还给人类带来 跨物种溢出 的风险。该研究旨在探索干扰素在鸟类中诱导的 免疫应答 对 IAV 的影响,因为这一领域不像人类那样被充分了解。
设计与对象
该研究使用鸡、鹌鹑和鸭细胞来鉴定干扰素刺激基因(ISG)并评估它们在限制 IAV 复制中的作用。它重点关注两种 IAV 毒株:小鼠适应性 PR8 和低致病性禽 IAV 分离株 WF10。
核心方法
针对两种 IAV 毒株 PR8 和 WF10 进行了 CRISPR 敲除 ISG 筛选,以鉴定限制病毒复制的基因。该研究利用鸡、鹌鹑和鸭细胞来检测鸟类中干扰素诱导的 免疫应答。
主要结果
对于 PR8,IFITM3 被确定为病毒复制的主要限制因子,另外几次点击显示出适度的影响。对于禽类 WF10,IFITM3 也很受欢迎,但发现另外两个基因 BAG5 和 OTUD4 限制复制,而 PR8 结果中不存在这两个基因。
解释与创新
这些发现证实了物种间干扰素介导的免疫的差异,并强调了物种特异性 ISG 在限制 IAV 复制中的重要性。本研究建立了一个筛选系统来识别与鸟类相关的 ISG。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对禽类针对 IAV 的先天免疫机制的深入了解,这可以为 监测、家禽种群中 禽流感 的预防和控制策略提供信息,并了解人畜共患 传播 的潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的报告,不提供详细的统计分析或实验验证。由于缺乏关于已确定的 ISG 在鸟类物种中的功能作用的全面数据,证据强度中等。

A chicken interferon-stimulated gene screen reveals limited genes restricting influenza virus replication

Authors: Alison J. Barkhymer, Jordan T. Becker, Frances K. Shepherd, Clayton K. Mickelson, Lauren M. Pross, Mark Salnikov, Ryan Andrew Langlois
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Avian influenza A virus (IAV) is a major global health threat. Not only does it cause significant economic losses to the agricultural industry, but it also presents a risk of spillover into the human population. However, several obstacles must be overcome for a successful cross-species transmission event to occur. One of these obstacles is the innate immunity of the host. While the innate immune response to IAV in humans has been well-studied, the innate immune response to IAV in birds is not as well understood. Here, we explore the interferon-induced immune response by identifying interferon-stimulated genes (ISGs) in chicken, quail, and duck cells, and find that they are largely species-specific. Using this data, we performed a CRISPR knockout ISG screen against two strains of IAV, the mouse-adapted PR8, and the low pathogenicity avian IAV isolate WF10. We find that for PR8, IFITM3 is the dominant restrictor of viral replication with several additional hits with modest effects, consistent with previous work in mammals. For avian WF10, IFITM3 was also a hit; however, there were two additional hits absent for PR8, the HSP70 co-chaperone protein BAG5 and the deubiquitinase OTUD4. Overall, these findings confirm the divergence of interferon-mediated immunity between species and establish a screening system that can be used to identify ISGs important for viral restriction in avian species. IMPORTANCE Wild birds, especially waterfowl and shorebirds, are the primary natural reservoir for influenza A viruses. These birds can transmit IAV to domestic poultry, where the spillover risk to humans increases. Chickens are the most common species of domestic poultry worldwide and frequently infected with avian influenza viruses. Despite this, relatively little is known about how their immune system responds to influenza infection. This lack of knowledge creates a barrier to finding ways of preventing or mitigating IAV infection in chicken flocks. In this study, we characterized the interferon-induced immune response to IAV in chickens and found that only a small subset of ISGs were responsible for restricting IAV in chicken cells.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza A virus (IAV) is a major global health threat, not only causing significant economic losses to the agricultural industry but also presenting a risk of spillover into the human population. The study aimed to explore the interferon-induced immune response to IAV in birds, as this area is not as well understood as in humans.
Design and population
The study was conducted using chicken, quail, and duck cells to identify interferon-stimulated genes (ISGs) and assess their role in restricting IAV replication. It focused on two strains of IAV: the mouse-adapted PR8 and the low pathogenicity avian IAV isolate WF10.
Core methods
A CRISPR knockout ISG screen was performed against two strains of IAV, PR8 and WF10, to identify genes that restrict viral replication. The study utilized chicken, quail, and duck cells to examine the interferon-induced immune response in avian species.
Main results
For PR8, IFITM3 was identified as the dominant restrictor of viral replication, with several additional hits showing modest effects. For avian WF10, IFITM3 was also a hit, but two additional genes, BAG5 and OTUD4, were found to restrict replication, which were absent in the PR8 results.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings confirm the divergence of interferon-mediated immunity between species and highlight the importance of species-specific ISGs in restricting IAV replication. This study establishes a screening system for identifying ISGs relevant to avian species.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the innate immune mechanisms of avian species against IAV, which can inform strategies for surveillance, prevention, and control of avian influenza in poultry populations, as well as understanding the potential for zoonotic transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level reporting and does not provide detailed statistical analyses or experimental validation. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of comprehensive data on the functional roles of the identified ISGs in avian species.

具有血凝素柄的粘膜 疫苗接种 为小鼠提供针对流感病毒亚型的广泛保护

Mucosal vaccination with hemagglutinin stalk provides broad protection against influenza virus subtypes in mice
作者： Wei-Zhong Li, Arunraj Mekhemadhom Rajendrakumar, Zi-Zhen Miao, Bibek Dhakal, Bogdan Mikhailovich Domrachev, Tao Wang, Qiu-Qi Kan, Qiao-Feng Wan, Hong-Quan Wan, Wen-Bin Tuo
摘要中文翻译流感病毒通过抗原漂移和转移不断进化,通过呼吸道飞沫和气溶胶传播。开发通用鼻 疫苗 对于通过诱导呼吸道持久免疫力来预防季节性和大流行性感染至关重要。新生儿 Fc 受体 (FcRn) 将 IgG 抗体转移穿过上皮屏障。这项研究调查了 i.n. 是否利用 FcRn 递送 HA 的保守 Stalk 抗原,可以刺激 免疫应答 针对各种流感病毒亚型的保护性。我们设计了一种单体 IgG Fc,以预融合形式与经过修饰的 HA Stalk 融合。与对照组小鼠相比,用 Stalk-Fc 免疫的小鼠表现出显着的针对 H1N1、H5N1、H6N2 和 H9N2 感染的保护作用。免疫小鼠在鼻腔冲洗液、支气管肺泡灌洗液和血液中产生持久的 HA Stalk 特异性 IgA 和 IgG Ab,以及肺部的组织驻留记忆 T 细胞和骨髓中的长寿命浆细胞。这导致鼻甲和肺部的病毒复制显着降低。Stalk 特异性抗体通过中和、抗体或补体依赖性细胞毒性或吞噬作用,表现出针对不同流感亚型的强烈交叉反应性。这些结果表明 FcRn 有效转运 HA Stalk-Fc 穿过呼吸道上皮屏障,支持通用流感鼻 疫苗 的开发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种针对流感病毒的通用鼻疫苗,流感病毒通过抗原漂移和转移不断进化,通过呼吸道飞沫和气溶胶传播。开发这样的 疫苗 对于通过诱导呼吸道的持久免疫力来预防季节性和大流行性感染至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究研究了小鼠中具有保守的血凝素 (HA) Stalk 抗原的粘膜 疫苗接种,利用新生儿 Fc 受体 (FcRn) 将 IgG 抗体转移穿过上皮屏障。
核心方法
研究人员设计了一种单体 IgG Fc,以预融合形式与经过修饰的 HA Stalk 融合,并用这种结构对小鼠进行免疫。
主要结果
与对照小鼠相比,免疫小鼠对 H1N1、H5N1、H6N2 和 H9N2 感染表现出显着的保护作用。免疫小鼠在鼻腔冲洗液、支气管肺泡灌洗液和血液中产生持久的HA Stalk特异性IgA和IgG抗体,以及肺部组织驻留的记忆T细胞和骨髓中长寿命的浆细胞,导致鼻甲和肺部的病毒复制显着降低。
解释与创新
结果表明,FcRn 有效转运 HA Stalk-Fc 穿过呼吸道上皮屏障,支持通用流感鼻 疫苗 的开发。Stalk 特异性抗体通过中和、抗体或补体依赖性细胞毒性或吞噬作用,表现出针对不同流感亚型的强烈交叉反应性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发针对各种流感病毒亚型的通用鼻用 疫苗 提供信息,从而增强 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性包括使用小鼠作为模型,这可能无法完全推广到人类。由于缺乏直接的人体试验,证据强度中等。

Mucosal vaccination with hemagglutinin stalk provides broad protection against influenza virus subtypes in mice

Authors: Wei-Zhong Li, Arunraj Mekhemadhom Rajendrakumar, Zi-Zhen Miao, Bibek Dhakal, Bogdan Mikhailovich Domrachev, Tao Wang, Qiu-Qi Kan, Qiao-Feng Wan, Hong-Quan Wan, Wen-Bin Tuo
Original abstract (English)
Influenza viruses continually evolve through antigenic drift and shift, spreading via respiratory droplets and aerosols. Developing a universal nasal vaccine is essential for preventing seasonal and pandemic infections by inducing long-lasting immunity in the respiratory tract. The neonatal Fc receptor (FcRn) transfers IgG Abs across the epithelial barrier. This study investigates whether the i.n. delivery of the conserved Stalk antigen of HA, utilizing the FcRn, can stimulate protective immune responses against various influenza virus subtypes. We engineered a monomeric IgG Fc fused to a modified HA Stalk in its prefusion form. immunized with Stalk-Fc exhibited significant protection against infections from H1N1, H5N1, H6N2, and H9N2 compared with groups of control mice. The immunized mice produced long-lasting HA Stalk–specific IgA and IgG Abs in nasal washes, bronchoalveolar lavage, and blood, along with tissue-resident memory T cells in the lungs and long-lived plasma cells in the bone marrow. This led to notably lower viral replication in the nasal turbinate and lungs. The Stalk-specific Abs demonstrated strong cross-reactivity against different influenza subtypes through neutralization, Ab- or complement-dependent cellular cytotoxicity, or phagocytosis. These results indicate that FcRn efficiently transports HA Stalk-Fc across the respiratory epithelial barrier, supporting the development of a universal influenza nasal vaccine.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a universal nasal vaccine against influenza viruses, which continually evolve through antigenic drift and shift, spreading via respiratory droplets and aerosols. Developing such a vaccine is essential for preventing seasonal and pandemic infections by inducing long-lasting immunity in the respiratory tract.
Design and population
The study investigated the mucosal vaccination with the conserved Stalk antigen of hemagglutinin (HA) in mice, utilizing the neonatal Fc receptor (FcRn) to transfer IgG Abs across the epithelial barrier.
Core methods
The researchers engineered a monomeric IgG Fc fused to a modified HA Stalk in its prefusion form and immunized mice with this construct.
Main results
Immunized mice exhibited significant protection against infections from H1N1, H5N1, H6N2, and H9N2 compared with control mice. The immunized mice produced long-lasting HA Stalk–specific IgA and IgG Abs in nasal washes, bronchoalveolar lavage, and blood, along with tissue-resident memory T cells in the lungs and long-lived plasma cells in the bone marrow, leading to notably lower viral replication in the nasal turbinate and lungs.
Interpretation and novelty
The results indicate that FcRn efficiently transports HA Stalk-Fc across the respiratory epithelial barrier, supporting the development of a universal influenza nasal vaccine. The Stalk-specific Abs demonstrated strong cross-reactivity against different influenza subtypes through neutralization, Ab- or complement-dependent cellular cytotoxicity, or phagocytosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform the development of a universal nasal vaccine against various influenza virus subtypes, enhancing surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations include the use of mice as a model, which may not fully generalize to humans. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of direct human trials.

2024-2025 年期间,中国出现了具有快速 进化 和抗原分歧的 2.3.4.4b H5 病毒的独特进化簇。

Emergence of a distinct evolutionary cluster of clade 2.3.4.4b H5 viruses in China during 2024-2025 with rapid evolution and antigenic divergence.
作者： Xinkui Zhang, Yujia Yang, Xirong Huang, Shunyin Fang, Xinyu Han, Dandan Wei, Jianghui Zou, Beibei Niu, Huifang Yin, Ming Liao
摘要中文翻译进化枝2.3.4.4b H5病毒自在中国出现以来经历了连续的进化,但最近流行的病毒的遗传和抗原多样性程度仍不清楚。在这里,我们对 2024 年至 2025 年期间在中国通过常规 监测 分离的 18 种 H5 病毒进行了特征分析,以及自 2020 年以来收集的公开的进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5 序列。系统发育分析表明,这些分离株在进化枝 2.3.4.4b 内形成了一个独特的进化簇,称为 G2 衍生的新兴簇 (G2-EC),于 2024 年中期左右出现,与之前流行的 H5N1 病毒表现出明显的遗传差异。G2-EC病毒积累了一组特征性的血凝素氨基酸取代,其中大部分位于抗原区域。在分支 2.3.4.4b H5 病毒群体中,2024-2025 年期间估计的平均 HA 进化率高于之前时期。这些基因变化伴随着与 Re14 疫苗 菌株的显着抗原差异,HI 滴度降低了 4 至 32 倍。基因组分析显示,G2-EC H5N1 病毒从不同的遗传背景中获得了 PB1 片段,随后出现了共享高度相似的遗传主链但携带不同 NA 片段的 H5N6 病毒,这表明存在连续重配。动物实验表明,代表性的G2-EC病毒在SPF鸡中保留了高致病性和高效的直接接触传播,而在小鼠中表现出相对有限的致死率。总的来说,这些发现记录了中国分支 2.3.4.4b H5 病毒最近的进化转变,其特征是遗传和抗原分歧伴随着顺序重配,强调了持续基因组和抗原 监测 的需要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
进化枝2.3.4.4b H5病毒自在中国出现以来经历了连续的进化,但最近流行的病毒的遗传和抗原多样性程度仍不清楚。
设计与对象
在这里,我们对 2024-2025 年期间在中国通过常规 监测 分离的 18 种 H5 病毒进行了特征分析,以及自 2020 年以来收集的公开可用的进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5 序列。
核心方法
系统发育分析显示,这些分离株在进化枝 2.3.4.4b 内形成了一个独特的进化簇,称为 G2 衍生新兴簇 (G2-EC),该簇于 2024 年中期左右出现,与之前流行的 H5N1 病毒表现出明显的遗传差异。
主要结果
G2-EC病毒积累了一组特征性的血凝素氨基酸取代,其中大部分位于抗原区域。
解释与创新
在分支 2.3.4.4b H5 病毒群体中,2024-2025 年期间估计的平均 HA 进化率高于之前时期。
科研与公卫意义
这些基因变化伴随着与 Re14 疫苗 菌株的显着抗原差异,HI 滴度降低了 4 至 32 倍。
局限与证据强度
基因组分析显示,G2-EC H5N1 病毒从不同的遗传背景中获得了 PB1 片段,随后出现了共享高度相似的遗传主链但携带不同 NA 片段的 H5N6 病毒,这表明存在连续重配。

Emergence of a distinct evolutionary cluster of clade 2.3.4.4b H5 viruses in China during 2024-2025 with rapid evolution and antigenic divergence.

Authors: Xinkui Zhang, Yujia Yang, Xirong Huang, Shunyin Fang, Xinyu Han, Dandan Wei, Jianghui Zou, Beibei Niu, Huifang Yin, Ming Liao
Original abstract (English)
Clade 2.3.4.4b H5 viruses have undergone continuous evolution since their emergence in China, but the extent of genetic and antigenic diversification among recently circulating viruses remains unclear. Here, we characterized 18 H5 viruses isolated through routine surveillance in China during 2024-2025, together with publicly available clade 2.3.4.4b H5 sequences collected since 2020. Phylogenetic analysis revealed that these isolates formed a distinct evolutionary cluster within clade 2.3.4.4b, designated the G2-derived emerging cluster (G2-EC), which emerged around mid-2024 and exhibited clear genetic divergence from previously circulating H5N1 viruses. G2-EC viruses accumulated a characteristic set of hemagglutinin amino acid substitutions, most of which were located in antigenic regions. Across the clade 2.3.4.4b H5 virus population, the estimated mean HA evolutionary rate was higher during 2024-2025 than during preceding periods. These genetic changes were accompanied by marked antigenic divergence from the Re14 vaccine strain, with 4- to 32-fold reductions in HI titres. Genomic analysis revealed acquisition of PB1 segment from a distinct genetic background in G2-EC H5N1 viruses, followed by the emergence of H5N6 viruses sharing a highly similar genetic backbone but carrying a distinct NA segment, indicating sequential reassortment. Animal experiments showed that representative G2-EC viruses retained high pathogenicity and efficient direct-contact transmission in SPF chickens, while exhibiting relatively limited lethality in mice. Collectively, these findings document a recent evolutionary transition of clade 2.3.4.4b H5 viruses in China, characterized by genetic and antigenic divergence accompanied by sequential reassortment, underscoring the need for continued genomic and antigenic surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Clade 2.3.4.4b H5 viruses have undergone continuous evolution since their emergence in China, but the extent of genetic and antigenic diversification among recently circulating viruses remains unclear.
Design and population
Here, we characterized 18 H5 viruses isolated through routine surveillance in China during 2024-2025, together with publicly available clade 2.3.4.4b H5 sequences collected since 2020.
Core methods
Phylogenetic analysis revealed that these isolates formed a distinct evolutionary cluster within clade 2.3.4.4b, designated the G2-derived emerging cluster (G2-EC), which emerged around mid-2024 and exhibited clear genetic divergence from previously circulating H5N1 viruses.
Main results
G2-EC viruses accumulated a characteristic set of hemagglutinin amino acid substitutions, most of which were located in antigenic regions.
Interpretation and novelty
Across the clade 2.3.4.4b H5 virus population, the estimated mean HA evolutionary rate was higher during 2024-2025 than during preceding periods.
Scientific and public-health significance
These genetic changes were accompanied by marked antigenic divergence from the Re14 vaccine strain, with 4- to 32-fold reductions in HI titres.
Limitations and evidence strength
Genomic analysis revealed acquisition of PB1 segment from a distinct genetic background in G2-EC H5N1 viruses, followed by the emergence of H5N6 viruses sharing a highly similar genetic backbone but carrying a distinct NA segment, indicating sequential reassortment.

A(H1N1)pdm09 感染后针对甲型流感 (H5) 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒的交叉保护。

Cross-Protection against Influenza A(H5) Clade 2.3.4.4b Viruses after A(H1N1)pdm09 Infection.
作者： Xiangjie Sun, Jessica A Belser, Zhu-Nan Li, Nicole Brock, Joanna A Pulit-Penaloza, Troy J Kieran, Claudia Pappas, Hui Zeng, Jessie C Chang, Paul J Carney
摘要中文翻译我们用 H5N1 或 H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒对先前感染过 A(H1N1)pdm09 流感病毒的雪貂进行攻击,以评估交叉保护作用。结果显示,之前的 A(H1N1)pdm09 病毒免疫对这两种病毒具有部分保护作用,与 H5N1 相比,对 H5N5 的保护作用略有减弱,这可能反映了不存在交叉反应性 N5 神经氨酸酶抗体。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估既往感染甲型(H1N1)pdm09 流感病毒是否可以提供针对 H5N1 和 H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒的交叉保护,从而解决有关 免疫应答 到 禽流感 亚型的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究涉及用 H5N1 和 H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒攻击先前感染过 A(H1N1)pdm09 流感病毒的雪貂,以评估动物模型中的交叉保护作用。
核心方法
雪貂感染甲型(H1N1)pdm09 流感病毒,然后用 H5N1 或 H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒攻击,以评估 免疫应答 和病毒复制。
主要结果
结果显示,之前的 A(H1N1)pdm09 病毒免疫对 H5N1 和 H5N5 病毒均具有部分保护作用,与 H5N1 相比,对 H5N5 的保护作用略有减弱,这可能是由于缺乏交叉反应性 N5 神经氨酸酶抗体。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,A(H1N1)pdm09 感染的免疫力可能对 H5N1 和 H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒提供一定的交叉保护,突出了 禽流感 亚型中免疫交叉反应的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为 监测 和 禽流感 亚型的风险评估策略提供信息,特别是在了解 免疫应答 和人群中潜在的交叉保护方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于动物模型,证据基于单一挑战实验,可能无法完全代表人类 免疫应答。证据的强度中等。

Cross-Protection against Influenza A(H5) Clade 2.3.4.4b Viruses after A(H1N1)pdm09 Infection.

Authors: Xiangjie Sun, Jessica A Belser, Zhu-Nan Li, Nicole Brock, Joanna A Pulit-Penaloza, Troy J Kieran, Claudia Pappas, Hui Zeng, Jessie C Chang, Paul J Carney
Original abstract (English)
We challenged ferrets previously infected with influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 virus with H5N1 or H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses to assess cross-protection. Results show that previous A(H1N1)pdm09 virus immunity conferred partial protection against both viruses, with slightly reduced protection against H5N5 compared with H5N1, potentially reflecting the absence of cross-reactive N5 neuraminidase antibodies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess whether prior infection with influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 virus provides cross-protection against H5N1 and H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, addressing a knowledge gap about immune responses to avian influenza subtypes.
Design and population
The study involved challenging ferrets previously infected with influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 virus with H5N1 and H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses to evaluate cross-protection in an animal model.
Core methods
Ferrets were infected with influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 virus and then challenged with H5N1 or H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses to assess immune responses and viral replication.
Main results
Results showed that previous A(H1N1)pdm09 virus immunity conferred partial protection against both H5N1 and H5N5 viruses, with slightly reduced protection against H5N5 compared to H5N1, potentially due to the absence of cross-reactive N5 neuraminidase antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that immunity from A(H1N1)pdm09 infection may offer some cross-protection against H5N1 and H5N5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, highlighting the potential for immune cross-reactivity in avian influenza subtypes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies for avian influenza subtypes, particularly in understanding immune responses and potential cross-protection in human populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an animal model, and the evidence is based on a single challenge experiment, which may not fully represent human immune responses. The evidence is moderate in strength.

探索戴口罩驱动因素的离散选择实验

A Discrete Choice Experiment to Explore Drivers of Mask-Wearing
作者： Katherine M. LaCasse
摘要中文翻译离散选择实验将研究疾病因素、政策和社会因素如何影响假设的禽流感 暴发 期间戴口罩的决定。具体来说,它将探讨五个不同因素(感染率、死亡率 率、口罩要求、社会规范、弱势群体的存在)对个人佩戴医用口罩决定的影响。由于最近美国各地牲畜中出现禽流感疫情,许多卫生专业人员担心禽流感病毒可能发生突变,导致人类出现大规模禽流感疫情。因此,我们设计了我们的研究,要求参与者报告在这种假设的 暴发 禽流感期间,他们在不同情况下戴口罩的可能性有多大。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨在假设的禽流感 暴发 期间影响个人决定佩戴医用口罩的因素,解决了解疾病特征、政策措施和社会规范如何影响佩戴口罩行为的需要。
设计与对象
该研究采用离散选择实验来评估参与者在各种情况下戴口罩的可能性,重点关注个人对涉及禽流感 暴发 的假设情况的反应。
核心方法
该研究涉及设计场景,其中包括感染率、死亡率 率、口罩要求、社会规范和弱势群体的存在等因素,参与者报告他们在这些情况下戴口罩的可能性。
主要结果
参与者被要求报告他们在不同情况下戴口罩的可能性,但所提供的证据中并未提供确切的结果,例如百分比或 P 值。
解释与创新
作者认为,了解这些因素可以为公共卫生策略提供信息,但该研究没有提供有关禽流感背景下戴口罩行为的具体解释或新发现。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于制定有针对性的公共卫生干预措施,旨在促进潜在禽流感 暴发 期间佩戴口罩,尽管该研究没有报告对 暴发 或预防的具体影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于假设场景,并未提供真实 暴发 的实际数据,这限制了证据的强度及其对现实世界环境的适用性。

A Discrete Choice Experiment to Explore Drivers of Mask-Wearing

Authors: Katherine M. LaCasse
Original abstract (English)
A discrete choice experiment will examine how disease factors, policy, and social factors influence mask-wearing decisions during a hypothetical bird flu outbreak. Specifically, it will explore the influence of five different factors (infection rate, mortality rate, mask mandate, social norm, presence of vulnerable others) on individuals’ decision to wear a medical mask. Due to recent outbreaks of bird flu among livestock across the US, many health professionals are concerned about the potential of the bird flu virus to mutate leading to a large outbreak among humans. Therefore, we designed our study asking participants to report how likely they would be to wear a mask in different scenarios during such a hypothetical outbreak of bird flu.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the factors influencing individuals’ decisions to wear medical masks during a hypothetical bird flu outbreak, addressing the need to understand how disease characteristics, policy measures, and social norms might affect mask-wearing behavior.
Design and population
The study employed a discrete choice experiment to assess participants’ likelihood of wearing masks in various scenarios, focusing on individuals’ responses to hypothetical situations involving bird flu outbreaks.
Core methods
The study involved designing scenarios that included factors such as infection rate, mortality rate, mask mandate, social norm, and presence of vulnerable others, with participants reporting their likelihood of mask-wearing in these contexts.
Main results
Participants were asked to report their likelihood of wearing a mask in different scenarios, but the exact results, such as percentages or P values, are not provided in the supplied evidence.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that understanding these factors could inform public health strategies, but the study does not provide specific interpretations or novel findings about mask-wearing behavior in the context of bird flu.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could contribute to the development of targeted public health interventions aimed at promoting mask-wearing during potential bird flu outbreaks, though the study does not report specific implications for surveillance or prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on hypothetical scenarios and does not provide actual data from real outbreaks, which limits the strength of the evidence and its applicability to real-world settings.

通过参与式科学近乎实时地对野生动物进行全球监测 死亡率

Global monitoring of wildlife mortality through participatory science in near‐real time
作者： Diego Ellis‐Soto, Liam U. Taylor, Elizabeth Edson, Avery P. Hill, Christopher J. Schell, Carl Boettiger, Rebecca Fay Johnson
摘要中文翻译野生动物 死亡率 事件的检测对于及时保护和自然资源管理至关重要。我们提出了一个基于网络的开源决策支持工具,可以查询、聚合和总结来自 iNaturalist 的参与式科学数据,以监控全球的 死亡率 事件。我们使用涵盖分类、空间和时间尺度的四个案例研究证明了这种方法的有效性。在加拿大和美国,鸟类 死亡率 的高峰与 禽流感 暴发 期间的人畜共患病风险一致。2026 年 3 月,我们在非洲各地检测到 45 起受威胁物种的 死亡率 事件。在加利福尼亚州,记录的哺乳动物 死亡率 与人类基础设施有关,包括靠近道路,以及较小程度上的人类足迹。在 12 个国家检测到美洲狮 (Puma concolor) 的 死亡率,凸显了国际合作保护流动物种的必要性。我们的工具使资源管理者能够标记新出现的威胁,并使参与科学家能够监控和整合 死亡率 记录以进行保护。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决及时发现野生动物 死亡率 事件以支持保护和自然资源管理的迫切需求。作者试图开发并演示一种在全球范围内监测这些事件的工具。
设计与对象
该研究采用参与式科学方法,利用 iNaturalist 的数据来监测不同地区和物种的 死亡率 事件。它包括四个案例研究,涵盖各种分类、空间和时间尺度。
核心方法
作者创建了一个基于网络的开源决策支持工具,用于查询、聚合和总结来自 iNaturalist 的参与式科学数据。他们使用这个工具来分析特定地区和物种的 死亡率 事件。
主要结果
在加拿大和美国,鸟类 死亡率 的高峰与 禽流感 暴发 期间的人畜共患病风险一致。2026 年 3 月,在非洲各地检测到 45 起受威胁物种 死亡率 事件。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了参与式科学在识别与人畜共患病风险相关的野生动物 死亡率 模式方面的潜力以及监测受威胁物种的重要性。该工具在检测这些事件方面的有效性是对保护实践的新颖贡献。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过识别新出现的威胁以及保护流动物种方面国际合作的必要性来为保护战略提供信息。它们还有助于了解与 禽流感 暴发 相关的人畜共患病风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对参与性科学数据的依赖而受到限制,这些数据可能存在偏差或差距。证据是中等的,因为它基于来自单一平台的汇总数据,并且不包括详细的实验室或统计分析。

Global monitoring of wildlife mortality through participatory science in near‐real time

Authors: Diego Ellis‐Soto, Liam U. Taylor, Elizabeth Edson, Avery P. Hill, Christopher J. Schell, Carl Boettiger, Rebecca Fay Johnson
Original abstract (English)
Detection of wildlife mortality events is critical for timely conservation and natural resource management. We present an open‐source, web‐based, decision‐support tool that queries, aggregates, and summarizes participatory‐science data from iNaturalist to monitor mortality events worldwide. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this approach using four case studies spanning taxonomic, spatial, and temporal scales. In Canada and the US, high peaks of bird mortality coincided with zoonotic risk during avian influenza outbreaks. Across Africa, we detected 45 mortality events of threatened species in March 2026. In California, recorded mammal mortality was associated with human infrastructure, including proximity to roads, and to a lesser extent, the human footprint. Mortality of pumas ( Puma concolor ) was detected across 12 countries, highlighting the need for international cooperation to conserve mobile species. Our tool enables resource managers to flag emerging threats and empowers participatory scientists to monitor and integrate mortality records for conservation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the critical need for timely detection of wildlife mortality events to support conservation and natural resource management. The authors sought to develop and demonstrate a tool for monitoring these events globally.
Design and population
The study employed a participatory science approach, utilizing data from iNaturalist to monitor mortality events across different regions and species. It included four case studies covering various taxonomic, spatial, and temporal scales.
Core methods
The authors created an open-source, web-based decision-support tool that queries, aggregates, and summarizes participatory science data from iNaturalist. They used this tool to analyze mortality events in specific regions and species.
Main results
In Canada and the US, high peaks of bird mortality coincided with zoonotic risk during avian influenza outbreaks. Across Africa, 45 mortality events of threatened species were detected in March 2026.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the potential of participatory science in identifying patterns of wildlife mortality linked to zoonotic risks and the importance of monitoring threatened species. The tool's effectiveness in detecting these events is a novel contribution to conservation practices.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform conservation strategies by identifying emerging threats and the need for international cooperation in protecting mobile species. They also contribute to understanding zoonotic risks associated with avian influenza outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on participatory science data, which may have biases or gaps. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on aggregated data from a single platform and does not include detailed laboratory or statistical analyses.

乍得恩贾梅纳活禽市场生物安全措施评估

Assessment of Biosecurity Measures in Live Poultry Markets in N’Djamena, Chad
作者： Ousmane ISSA ABDEL DJALIL, Brahim Béchir Ali, Ahmota Romain Daiba, Seid Gamane Adoum
摘要中文翻译背景和目标:尽管生物安全是畜牧业疾病预防的主要手段,但在活家禽供应链中实施这些措施仍然具有挑战性。这项研究评估了乍得恩贾梅纳四个主要商业市场(Al-Afia、Champs de Fil、Diguel 和 Dembé)生物安全措施的实施情况。方法:2023 年 7 月至 8 月期间,对 120 个固定活家禽摊贩进行了横断面调查。使用结构化调查问卷来评估供应商对禽类病原体的认识、生物安全标准的知识和卫生实践的实施情况。结果:结果显示,60.83%的商贩生物安全知识水平较低,23.34%的商户生物安全知识中等,15.83%的商户生物安全知识良好。Dembé 市场拥有良好生物安全知识的供应商比例最高,占受访者总数的 6.67%,Mean±SD 为 6.67±0.97%,显着高于 Al-Afia(2.50±0.31%)和 Diguel(1.66±0.09%)的相应值。在合规性方面,60% 的供应商未能实施生物安全协议,而只有 5% 的供应商严格遵守建议的标准。与 Champs de Fil (10.83±2.87%) 相比,完全不执行的情况在 Diguel (17.50±4.28%) 和 Al-Afia (15.00±3.44%) 最为普遍。这些缺陷与缺乏正规培训、市场基础设施不足以及对直接健康风险的认识较低有关。结论:调查结果表明恩贾梅纳活家禽供应商在生物安全知识和合规性方面存在巨大差距。鉴于活禽市场在新城疫和其他传染病中的潜在作用,迫切需要有针对性的培训计划、基础设施改善和强化监管框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估乍得恩贾梅纳活家禽市场生物安全措施的实施情况,因为生物安全是畜牧业疾病预防的主要手段,但这些措施在活家禽供应链中仍然具有挑战性。
设计与对象
对乍得恩贾梅纳四个主要商业市场(Al-Afia、Champs de Fil、Diguel 和 Dembé)的 120 个固定活家禽供应商进行了横断面调查。
核心方法
2023 年 7 月至 8 月期间,使用结构化调查问卷评估供应商对禽类病原体的认识、生物安全标准的了解以及卫生实践的实施情况。
主要结果
结果显示,60.83%的商户生物安全知识水平较低,23.34%的商户生物安全知识水平一般,15.83%的商户生物安全知识水平较高。Dembé 市场拥有良好生物安全知识的供应商比例最高,占受访者总数的 6.67%,Mean±SD 为 6.67±0.97%,显着高于 Al-Afia(2.50±0.31%)和 Diguel(1.66±0.09%)的相应值。在合规性方面,60% 的供应商未能实施生物安全协议,而只有 5% 的供应商严格遵守建议的标准。与 Champs de Fil (10.83±2.87%) 相比,完全不执行的情况在 Diguel (17.50±4.28%) 和 Al-Afia (15.00±3.44%) 最为普遍。
解释与创新
调查结果表明,恩贾梅纳的活家禽供应商在生物安全知识和合规性方面存在巨大差距,凸显了有针对性的培训计划、基础设施改进和强化监管框架的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究深入了解了活禽市场的生物安全实践,这对于预防 传播 和其他传染病至关重要,并强调了改进这些实践以保障公众健康的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横截面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。证据是温和的,因为它是基于调查结果而不是直接的生物学或临床测量。

Assessment of Biosecurity Measures in Live Poultry Markets in N’Djamena, Chad

Authors: Ousmane ISSA ABDEL DJALIL, Brahim Béchir Ali, Ahmota Romain Daiba, Seid Gamane Adoum
Original abstract (English)
Background and Objective: Although, biosecurity is a primary means of disease prevention in livestock farming, implementing these measures remains challenging in live poultry supply chains. This study evaluated the implementation of biosecurity measures across four major commercial markets in N'Djamena, Chad: Al-Afia, Champs de Fil, Diguel and Dembé. Methodology: Between July and August 2023, a cross-sectional survey was conducted among 120 permanent live poultry vendors. A structured questionnaire was used to assess vendors' awareness of avian pathogens, knowledge of biosecurity standards and implementation of sanitary practices. Results: The results showed that 60.83% of vendors had low levels of biosecurity knowledge, 23.34% had moderate knowledge and 15.83% had good knowledge. Dembé market had the highest proportion of vendors with good biosecurity knowledge, with 6.67% of the total respondents classified in this category and a Mean±SD of 6.67±0.97%, significantly higher than the corresponding values for Al-Afia (2.50±0.31%) and Diguel (1.66±0.09%). Regarding compliance, 60% of vendors failed to implement biosecurity protocols, whereas only 5% adhered strictly to the recommended standards. Complete non-implementation was most prevalent at Diguel (17.50±4.28%) and Al-Afia (15.00±3.44%), compared with Champs de Fil (10.83±2.87%). These deficiencies were associated with a lack of formal training, inadequate market infrastructure and low perceived immediate health risks. Conclusion: The findings indicate substantial gaps in biosecurity knowledge and compliance among live poultry vendors in N'Djamena. Given the potential role of live poultry markets in the transmission of avian influenza, Newcastle disease and other infectious diseases, targeted training programs, infrastructure improvements and strengthened regulatory frameworks are urgently needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the implementation of biosecurity measures in live poultry markets in N’Djamena, Chad, as biosecurity is a primary means of disease prevention in livestock farming, yet these measures remain challenging in live poultry supply chains.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted among 120 permanent live poultry vendors in four major commercial markets in N'Djamena, Chad: Al-Afia, Champs de Fil, Diguel and Dembé.
Core methods
A structured questionnaire was used to assess vendors' awareness of avian pathogens, knowledge of biosecurity standards and implementation of sanitary practices between July and August 2023.
Main results
The results showed that 60.83% of vendors had low levels of biosecurity knowledge, 23.34% had moderate knowledge and 15.83% had good knowledge. Dembé market had the highest proportion of vendors with good biosecurity knowledge, with 6.67% of the total respondents classified in this category and a Mean±SD of 6.67±0.97%, significantly higher than the corresponding values for Al-Afia (2.50±0.31%) and Diguel (1.66±0.09%). Regarding compliance, 60% of vendors failed to implement biosecurity protocols, whereas only 5% adhered strictly to the recommended standards. Complete non-implementation was most prevalent at Diguel (17.50±4.28%) and Al-Afia (15.00±3.44%), compared with Champs de Fil (10.83±2.87%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate substantial gaps in biosecurity knowledge and compliance among live poultry vendors in N'Djamena, highlighting the need for targeted training programs, infrastructure improvements and strengthened regulatory frameworks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the biosecurity practices in live poultry markets, which are critical for preventing the transmission of avian influenza and other infectious diseases, and underscores the importance of improving these practices to safeguard public health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate as it is based on survey results rather than direct biological or clinical measurements.

印度阿萨姆邦三级护理医院本科医学生关于单一健康和人畜共患疾病的知识、态度和实践:一项横断面研究

Knowledge, Attitude, and Practices Regarding One Health and Zoonotic Diseases Among Undergraduate Medical Students at a Tertiary Care Hospital in Assam, India: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Arunima Sarmah, Manas Kalita, Jutika Ojah
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人畜共患疾病是印度东北部的一个主要公共卫生问题,未来的医生需要 One Health 能力来控制这些疾病。我们评估了 MBBS 学生中的 One Health 和 人兽共患病 相关知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究(2026 年 9 月 11 日至 10 月 11 日)对阿萨姆邦高哈蒂医学院和医院所有三个阶段(第一阶段、第二阶段和第三阶段,最后一个阶段包括第一部分和第二部分)的 MBBS 学生进行了尝试性普查。
核心方法
经过试点的在线问卷评估了知识(20 项)、态度和实践(各 15 项);分数≥80%(修正的布鲁姆分界值)表明良好的知识、积极的态度或良好的实践。Kruskal-Wallis、卡方和多变量逻辑回归用于数据分析。
主要结果
在 881 名符合条件的学生中,有 512 名 (58.1%) 做出了回应(中位年龄 = 20 岁;四分位数范围 = 19-21 岁;333 名 (65.0%) 为男性);只有 37 名 (7.2%) 之前接受过 One Health 培训。分别有 352 名(68.8%)、269 名(52.5%)和 323 名(63.1%)学生拥有良好的知识、积极的态度和良好的实践。
解释与创新
知识和实践都相当不错,但态度是最薄弱的领域,特别是在第二阶段和第三阶段第一部分。对重点人畜共患病的了解有限。
科研与公卫意义
建议在所有阶段进行结构化的“同一个健康”教学,强调重点人畜共患疾病、与兽医学生的联合学习以及实地培训。大约三分之二(326 人,63.7%)支持将“同一个健康”纳入 MBBS 课程。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告与研究设计或数据分析相关的具体限制。由于研究的横截面性质以及自我报告数据的潜在偏差,置信度为中等。

Knowledge, Attitude, and Practices Regarding One Health and Zoonotic Diseases Among Undergraduate Medical Students at a Tertiary Care Hospital in Assam, India: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Arunima Sarmah, Manas Kalita, Jutika Ojah
Original abstract (English)
Background Zoonotic diseases are a major public health concern in Northeast India, and future physicians need One Health competencies to control them. We assessed One Health and zoonosis-related knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) among MBBS students. Methodology This cross-sectional study (September 11 to October 11, 2026) used an attempted census of MBBS students across all three phases (Phase I, Phase II, and Phase III, the last comprising Part I and Part II) at Gauhati Medical College and Hospital, Assam. A pilot-tested online questionnaire assessed knowledge (20 items), attitude, and practice (15 items each); scores ≥80% (modified Bloom’s cut-offs) indicated good knowledge, positive attitude, or good practice. Kruskal-Wallis, chi-square, and multivariable logistic regression were used for data analysis. Results Of the 881 eligible students, 512 (58.1%) responded (median age = 20 years; interquartile range = 19-21 years; 333 (65.0%) male); only 37 (7.2%) had previous One Health training. Good knowledge, positive attitude, and good practice were found in 352 (68.8%), 269 (52.5%), and 323 (63.1%) students, respectively. Knowledge was lowest for brucellosis (286, 55.9%), Nipah virus (331, 64.6%), and avian influenza (333, 65.0%). Protective practices were common, but fewer than half often or always attended seminars (243, 47.5%) or awareness activities (253, 49.4%). All domains differed significantly by phase: good knowledge and practice were highest in Phase III Part II (103, 86.6% and 89, 74.8%), and lowest in Phase II (68, 50.7% and 74, 55.2%), whereas positive attitude was highest in Phase I (134, 66.3%). Compared to Phase I, Phase III Part II students had higher odds of good knowledge (adjusted odds ratio (aOR) = 2.63, 95% confidence interval (CI): 1.39-4.99) and good practice (aOR = 1.81, 1.06-3.08; practice model p = 0.057), while all later phases had lower odds of positive attitude (aOR = 0.28-0.51). After excluding 51 (10.0%) students with uniform “strongly disagree” attitude responses, 269 (58.4%) of 461 had a positive attitude, and lower odds persisted for Phase II and Phase III Part I but not Phase III Part II. About two-thirds (326, 63.7%) supported including One Health in the MBBS curriculum. Conclusions Knowledge and practice were reasonably good, but attitude was the weakest domain, particularly in Phase II and Phase III Part I. Knowledge of priority zoonoses was limited. Structured One Health teaching across all phases, emphasizing priority zoonoses, joint learning with veterinary students, and field-based training, is recommended.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Zoonotic diseases are a major public health concern in Northeast India, and future physicians need One Health competencies to control them. We assessed One Health and zoonosis-related knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) among MBBS students.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study (September 11 to October 11, 2026) used an attempted census of MBBS students across all three phases (Phase I, Phase II, and Phase III, the last comprising Part I and Part II) at Gauhati Medical College and Hospital, Assam.
Core methods
A pilot-tested online questionnaire assessed knowledge (20 items), attitude, and practice (15 items each); scores ≥80% (modified Bloom’s cut-offs) indicated good knowledge, positive attitude, or good practice. Kruskal-Wallis, chi-square, and multivariable logistic regression were used for data analysis.
Main results
Of the 881 eligible students, 512 (58.1%) responded (median age = 20 years; interquartile range = 19-21 years; 333 (65.0%) male); only 37 (7.2%) had previous One Health training. Good knowledge, positive attitude, and good practice were found in 352 (68.8%), 269 (52.5%), and 323 (63.1%) students, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
Knowledge and practice were reasonably good, but attitude was the weakest domain, particularly in Phase II and Phase III Part I. Knowledge of priority zoonoses was limited.
Scientific and public-health significance
Structured One Health teaching across all phases, emphasizing priority zoonoses, joint learning with veterinary students, and field-based training, is recommended. About two-thirds (326, 63.7%) supported including One Health in the MBBS curriculum.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations related to the study design or data analysis. Confidence is moderate due to the cross-sectional nature of the study and potential biases in self-reported data.

可能性之空气:家禽中 传播 病毒经风传播的系统评价

An air of possibility: a systematic review of windborne transmission of avian influenza virus in poultry
作者： Sarah J. Robinson, Chelsea G. Himsworth, Kaylee A. Byers
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性 禽流感 病毒(HPAIV)对家禽生产、野生动物保护和公共卫生构成重大风险。虽然直接接触主要导致 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 在受感染鸡群中传播,但传入农场的情况更常见于间接途径,包括来自野生动物 储存宿主 和来自其他受感染场所的 传播 的环境污染。在可能的 传播 路径中,风载 传播 的潜力仍然是一个关键的知识差距。我们进行了系统评价,以评估目前对家禽中风传播 AIV 传播 的了解。我们使用与流感、家禽和风媒 传播 相关的术语检索了两个数据库。在确定的 414 篇手稿中,有 47 篇符合纳入标准。大多数研究是在荷兰 (n = 9) 和美国 (n = 9) 进行的。研究主要集中在商业环境中的高致病性 H5 菌株(n = 25)。大多数研究 ( n = 33) 是在 2014 年 HPAIV H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 出现后发表的。研究结果表明,风速和风向等环境因素可能会促进病毒传播数公里;然而,实验研究、现场调查和模型分析的结果并不一致。风载 传播 可能有助于 暴发 的动态,特别是在高密度生产区域,但其相对重要性仍不确定。多项研究建议将风参数纳入 暴发 调查并加强生物安全措施,例如空气过滤和风障。虽然 AIV 的风传播 传播 是一种看似合理且潜在重要的传播途径,但仍需要进一步研究来改进检测方法、完善预测模型,并解决将病毒检测与实际 传播 事件联系起来的实时现场数据中的差距。这将增强我们特别是高风险地区禽流感 暴发 的防控能力。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
禽流感 A 病毒、禽源人畜共患传染病和合格的禽亚型。生物相关的动物、分类学、模型、差异-诊断 和比较实体仅是补充性的,除非还存在材料目标特异性证据;只有显式的词汇或不相关的硬排除才是最终的。
证据基础与方法
我们进行了系统评价,以评估目前对家禽中风传播 AIV 传播 的了解。我们使用与流感、家禽和风媒 传播 相关的术语检索了两个数据库。
共识与结论
研究结果表明,风速和风向等环境因素可能会促进病毒传播数公里;然而,实验研究、现场调查和模型分析的结果并不一致。风载 传播 可能有助于 暴发 的动态,特别是在高密度生产区域,但其相对重要性仍不确定。
争议与缺口
虽然 AIV 的风传播 传播 是一种看似合理且潜在重要的传播途径,但仍需要进一步研究来改进检测方法、完善预测模型,并解决将病毒检测与实际 传播 事件联系起来的实时现场数据中的差距。
科研与实践启示
多项研究建议将风参数纳入 暴发 调查并加强生物安全措施,例如空气过滤和风障。这将增强我们特别是高风险地区禽流感 暴发 的防控能力。

An air of possibility: a systematic review of windborne transmission of avian influenza virus in poultry

Authors: Sarah J. Robinson, Chelsea G. Himsworth, Kaylee A. Byers
Original abstract (English)
Abstract High pathogenicity avian influenza virus (HPAIV) poses significant risks to poultry production, wildlife conservation, and public health. While direct contact primarily drives avian influenza virus (AIV) spread within infected flocks, introduction onto farms is more often attributed to indirect routes, including environmental contamination from wildlife reservoirs and transmission from other infected premises. Among plausible transmission pathways, the potential for windborne transmission remains a critical knowledge gap. We conducted a systematic review to assess current understanding of windborne AIV transmission in poultry. We searched two databases using terms related to influenza, poultry, and windborne transmission. Of 414 manuscripts identified, 47 met inclusion criteria. Most studies were conducted in the Netherlands ( n = 9) and the United States ( n = 9). Research primarily focused on high pathogenicity H5 strains ( n = 25) in commercial settings. The majority of studies ( n = 33) were published after 2014 following the emergence of HPAIV H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b. Findings suggest that environmental factors such as wind speed and direction may facilitate virus spread over several kilometers; however, findings were inconsistent across experimental studies, field investigations, and modelling analyses. Windborne transmission may contribute to outbreak dynamics, particularly in high-density production areas, but its relative importance remains uncertain. Several studies recommend incorporating wind parameters into outbreak investigations and strengthening biosecurity measures, such as air filtration and wind barriers. While windborne transmission of AIV is a plausible and potentially significant route of spread, further research is needed to improve detection methods, refine predictive models, and address gaps in real-time field data linking virus detection to actual transmission events. This will enhance our capacity to prevent and control AIV outbreaks, especially in high-risk regions.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Avian influenza A viruses, avian-origin zoonotic infections, and qualified avian subtypes. Biologically related animal, taxonomic, model, differential-diagnosis and comparative entities are supplementary-only unless material target-specific evidence is also present; only explicit lexical or unrelated hard exclusions are terminal.
Evidence base and method
We conducted a systematic review to assess current understanding of windborne AIV transmission in poultry. We searched two databases using terms related to influenza, poultry, and windborne transmission.
Consensus and conclusions
Findings suggest that environmental factors such as wind speed and direction may facilitate virus spread over several kilometers; however, findings were inconsistent across experimental studies, field investigations, and modelling analyses. Windborne transmission may contribute to outbreak dynamics, particularly in high-density production areas, but its relative importance remains uncertain.
Controversies and gaps
While windborne transmission of AIV is a plausible and potentially significant route of spread, further research is needed to improve detection methods, refine predictive models, and address gaps in real-time field data linking virus detection to actual transmission events.
Research and practice implications
Several studies recommend incorporating wind parameters into outbreak investigations and strengthening biosecurity measures, such as air filtration and wind barriers. This will enhance our capacity to prevent and control AIV outbreaks, especially in high-risk regions.

促进同一个健康的法律途径

Legal Approaches advancing One health
作者： Carmen Bullon, Francesca Coli
摘要中文翻译本文探讨了“同一个健康”方法在全球和欧盟 (EU) 层面的发展,认为监管视角对于其有效实施至关重要。在简要介绍之后,第二段概述了 One Health 的历史 进化,展示了其从主要卫生概念到完全融入环境维度的概念的进展。第三段分析了国际组织在促进和实施该方法方面的贡献。第四段讨论了立法在“统一健康”制度化中的作用,并回顾了不同的法律途径——从将其视为一项一般法律原则到将其纳入具体部门的立法以及采用全面的框架法——并以欧盟法律和政策的例子进行说明。第五段介绍了如何将“同一个健康”纳入法律体系的具体实例,特别是在 禽流感、生物多样性保护和农业食品系统领域,再次借鉴欧盟法规。该文件的结论是,没有任何单一的法律模式可以普遍实施“同一个健康”;相反,监管方法的选择必须适应每个国家或论坛的具体政策优先事项和体制背景。

本文探讨了“同一个健康”方法在全球和欧盟 (EU) 层面的发展,认为监管视角对于其有效实施至关重要。

在简要介绍之后,第二段概述了 One Health 的历史 进化,展示了其从主要卫生概念到完全融入环境维度的概念的进展。
共识与结论
第三段分析了国际组织在促进和实施该方法方面的贡献。

第四段讨论了立法在“统一健康”制度化中的作用,并回顾了不同的法律途径——从将其视为一项一般法律原则到将其纳入具体部门的立法以及采用全面的框架法——并以欧盟法律和政策的例子进行说明。

Legal Approaches advancing One health

Authors: Carmen Bullon, Francesca Coli
Original abstract (English)
This paper examines the development of the One Health approach at global and European Union (EU) levels, arguing that a regulatory perspective is essential for its effective implementation. Following a brief introduction, Paragraph II outlines the historical evolution of One Health, showing its progression from a predominantly sanitary concept to one that fully incorporates environmental dimensions. Paragraph III analyses the contribution of international organizations in promoting and operationalizing the approach. Paragraph IV discusses the role of legislation in institutionalizing One Health and reviews different legal pathways – ranging from its recognition as a general legal principle to its integration into sector specific legislation and the adoption of a comprehensive framework law – illustrated with examples from EU laws and policies. Paragraph V presents concrete instances of how One Health has been incorporated into legal systems, particularly in the areas of avian influenza, biodiversity conservation and agri-food systems, again drawing on EU regulation. The paper concludes that no single legal model can universally operationalize One Health; rather, the choice of regulatory approach must be adapted to the specific policy priorities and institutional context of each country or forum.

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
